## **PAGE BUILDER - COURSE SALES PAGE DOCUMENTATION**

### **DYNAMIC BLOCKS VS REGULAR BLOCKS**

#### **Dynamic Blocks (Header and Footer)**

**Characteristics**

* Standalone blocks not contained within other blocks
* Content pulled from site/course settings
* Label indicates "(Dynamic)" in toolbar
* Examples: Site Header, Site Footer

#### **Regular Content Blocks**

**Characteristics**

* Container blocks that hold multiple elements
* Can contain dynamic elements and static elements
* Blue outline when selected
* Example: Content Section Block (containing title, video, button)

\---

#### **Site Header Display**

**Location**: Top of page, independent dynamic block

   * State: Available
4. **Rounded Outline Button**

   * Style: Blue border outline
   * Corners: Fully rounded ends (pill shape)
   * Fill: Transparent/white
   * Text: Blue
   * State: Available
5. **Normal button with left icon**

   * Layout: Icon on left, text on right
   * Icon: White circle with blue background
   * Text: "Normal button with left icon"
   * Background: Solid blue
   * State: Available
6. **Normal button with right icon**

   * Icon: White circle with blue background
   * Text: "Normal button with right icon"
   * Background: Solid blue
   * State: Available
7. **Normal button with sub text**

   * Layout: Two-line text

   * Line 2: "subtext" (smaller, lighter text)
   * Background: Solid blue gradient
   * State: Available
8. **Outline button with icon left**

   * Layout: Icon on left, text on right
   * Icon: Blue circle outline
   * Text: "Outline button with icon left"
   * Border: Blue outline
   * Fill: Transparent/white
   * State: Available
9. **Outline button with icon right**

   * Layout: Text on left, icon on right
   * Icon: Blue circle outline
   * Text: "Outline button with icon right"
   * Border: Blue outline
   * Fill: Transparent/white
   * State: Available
10. **Outline button with sub text**

    * Layout: Two-line text
    * Line 1: "Outline button with sub text"
    * Line 2: "subtext" (smaller text)
    * Border: Blue outline
    * Fill: Transparent/white
    * State: Available
11. **Shadow Button**

    * Style: Similar to normal button
    * Effect: Drop shadow beneath button
    * Background: White/light
    * Text: Dark
    * State: Available
12. **3D Button** (First variant)

    * Style: Raised 3D effect
    * Background: Solid blue
    * Effect: Depth/layering appearance
    * State: Available
13. **3D Button** (Second variant)

    * Style: Raised 3D effect with stronger depth
    * Background: Solid blue
    * Effect: More pronounced 3D layering
    * State: Available

**Visual Layout**

* Display: Vertical scrolling list
* Spacing: Even gaps between options
* Interaction: Click to select style
* Preview: Full button rendered for each style

### **FREE PLAN CONFIGURATION**

#### **Section Title**

**Heading**: "Free Plan"

#### **Form Fields**

##### **Name**

* **Type**: Text input
* **Placeholder/Example**: "Beginners"
* **Width**: Full width

##### **Description**

* **Type**: Text input
* **Placeholder**: "description"
* **Width**: Full width

##### **Buy Button Text**

* **Type**: Text input
* **Info Icon**: (i) with tooltip
* **Tooltip**: "This text appears on the Buy button in the pricing plan block"
* **Placeholder/Value**: "Free"
* **Width**: Full width

##### **Add Bullet Points (Toggle - Default: OFF)**

* **Toggle**: Off / On
* **When ON, shows**:

  * **Bullet Points Title**

    * Placeholder: "What you will get in this plan?"
  * **Bullet Points** (with drag handle)

    * Placeholder: "Eg: You will get this in this course"
    * Actions: Copy, Delete icons
  * **Add Bullet Point** link (purple text)

##### **Set Enrollment Limit (Toggle - Default: OFF)**

* **Toggle**: Off / On
* **When ON, shows**:

  * **No. of Enrollments** (number input)

##### **Set Availability (Toggle - Default: OFF)**

* **Toggle**: Off / On
* **When ON, shows**:

  * **Enrollment Start Date**

    * Date \& Time picker + Timezone selector (side by side)
    * Helper text: "Date from which students can enroll in the course"
  * **Enrollment Close Date**

    * Date \& Time picker + Timezone selector (side by side)
    * Helper text: "Date till students can enroll in the course"
  * **Course Validity**

    * Number input + "days" unit
    * Helper text: "Course will be available from the date of enrollment till the days given here. For unlimited access set 0 as validity."

##### **Set Tiers (Toggle - Default: OFF)**

* **Toggle**: Off / On
* **Info Icon**: (i) with tooltip
* **Tooltip**: "Pricing can be set for tiers. Selected sections/lessons can only be accessed for this plan"
* **When ON, shows**:

  * **Sections/lessons**

    * Multi-select dropdown: "None selected"
    * Expanded view shows hierarchical checkboxes:

      * Section level: "Untitled Section" (collapsible)
      * Lesson level (indented): "Untitled lesson"

##### **Community**

* **Type**: Single select dropdown
* **Default**: "None"
* **Width**: Full width

##### **Make this as most popular plan**

* **Type**: Checkbox (unchecked)

#### **Footer Buttons**

* **Previous** (light purple)
* **Finish** (dark purple)

### **PLAN TYPE COMPARISON**

|Feature|Free|One Time Purchase|Subscription|Payment Plan|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|**Price**|No|Yes|Yes|Yes (Installment Amount)|
|**Tax Category**|No|Yes|Yes|Yes|
|**Additional Fee**|No|No|Yes|Yes|
|**Billing Frequency**|N/A|N/A|Yes|Yes|
|**Trial Days**|No|No|Yes|No|
|**Installments**|No|No|No|Yes (Number selector)|
|**Recurring Payment**|No|No|Yes|Yes|
|**Bullet Points**|Yes|Yes|Yes|Yes|
|**Enrollment Limit**|Yes|Yes|Yes|Yes|
|**Availability**|Yes|Yes|Yes|Yes|
|**Tiers**|Yes|Yes|Yes|Yes|
|**Community**|Yes|Yes|Yes|Yes|
|**Popular Plan**|Yes|Yes|Yes|Yes|

### **📧 EMAIL TEMPLATE EXAMPLES**

#### **Email 1: Access Instructions Email**

**Action Name:** Access Instructions Email  
**Timing:** After 0 Hours from Enrollment  
**Subject:** \[COURSE/SITE/PROGRAM] Access Details Inside.

**Content:**

```
\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\* Read \\\\\\\& Delete this block \\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*

- What is it? - This is Access Instructions Email Script. Drip ASAP.
- We are going to tell them how to access the course and what can they expect during the class.
- Do NOT set this email to Live until you edit it and make it yours.
- Edit/Delete the mail as you appropriate for your needs.
- Insert content in the short names in square brackets
- Also make sure to edit the links on this email
- Leave something in each mail for them to look forward to your next mail.

\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\* Read \\\\\\\& Delete this block \\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*

Hi \\\\\\\[first\\\\\\\_name],

Thank you for enrolling into my Course - \\\\\\\[COURSE/SITE/PROGRAM].

Today we start our 8 Week Journey to \\\\\\\[RESULTS].

#2 - Have You Joined our FB Group Yet?

Join the FB Group. Come and say "hi!"

Once again thank you for your joining in this journey with me. I am looking forward to helping you with what is needed to \\\\\\\[RESULTS].

Talk soon,

\\\\\\\[INSTRUCTOR NAME]

\\\\\\\[DESIGNATION]

\\\\\\\[#HASHTAG]
```

\---

#### **Email 2: Get them to revisit the Course again: Drip Day 2**

**Action Name:** Get them to revisit the Course again: Drip Day 2  
**Timing:** After 1 Days from Enrollment  
**Subject:** It's been 24 hours.

**Content:**

```
\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\* Read \\\\\\\& Delete this block \\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*

- What is it? - Get them to revisit the Course again Email Script - Drip: Day 2
- This is a way to draw them back into start/resuming the Course again as most people do not the start the course. Ie this is a way to get their attention again.
- Do NOT set this email to Live until you edit it and make it yours.
- Edit/Delete the mail as you appropriate for your needs.
- Also make sure to edit the links on this email

\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\* Read \\\\\\\& Delete this block \\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*

Hi \\\\\\\[first\\\\\\\_name],

I know it's only been 24 hours.

Did you know that 50% of students complete Section #1 within the first 24 hours - Have You?

Also, Did you know that those who move QUICKLY on this course get the best results out of this training?.

We want to help you get the BEST results you can get, so if you haven't already, commit to finishing Section #1 by end of today.

Section Name: \\\\\\\[SECTION #1]

In this section, I will be covering:

- LESSON #1: \\\\\\\[SECRET/REVEAL1]
- LESSON #2: \\\\\\\[SECRET/REVEAL2]
- LESSON #3: \\\\\\\[SECRET/REVEAL3]


PLEASE do NOT ignore Section #1, as the information in Section 1 will be the prerequisite for entire course.

I know we all get busy with life, but please set aside 30 minutes TODAY to finish this section #1 so that you are ready for the release of Section #2 in two days.


I'll be back tomorrow with even more information on \\\\\\\[NEXT SECTION]. For now, goto Section #1 and finish that.

See you there,

\\\\\\\[INSTRUCTOR NAME]

\\\\\\\[DESIGNATION]

\\\\\\\[#HASHTAG]
```

\---

#### **Email 3: Course Completion**

**Action Name:** Course Completion  
**Timing:** After 0 Hours from Course Completion  
**Subject:** Congratulations on Successfully Completing the \[COURSE/PRODUCT].

**Content:**

```
\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\* Read \\\\\\\& Delete this block \\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*\\\\\\\*

Talk soon,

\\\\\\\[INSTRUCTOR NAME]
```

```
\\\\\\\[SECTION #1] is LIVE now.

In this section, I will be covering:

- LESSON #1: \\\\\\\[SECRET/REVEAL1]
- LESSON #2: \\\\\\\[SECRET/REVEAL2]

ACTIVITY #2: Tweet \\\\\\\[@INSTRUCTOR] your number one takeaway from this section. Don't forget to add # \\\\\\\[HASHTAG] so we know it's from you in this course.

Start SECTION #1 HERE.


Talk soon,
```

### **🏷️ PERSONALIZATION TAGS**

#### **Merge Tag Styling**

* **Background:** Purple/blue highlight
* **Text Color:** White
* **Format:** Square brackets with tag name inside

#### **Common Merge Tags Used**

**Dynamic User Data:**

* **\[first\_name]** — Recipient's first name *(purple background)*

**Course/Product Information:**

* **\[COURSE/SITE/PROGRAM]** — Course or program name
* **\[COURSE/PRODUCT]** — Course or product reference
* **\[SECTION #1]** — Section identifier
* **\[NEXT SECTION]** — Upcoming content reference

**Content Placeholders:**

* **\[RESULTS]** — Desired outcome/transformation
* **\[SECRET/REVEAL1/2/3]** — Lesson content teasers

**Instructor Information:**

* **\[INSTRUCTOR NAME]** — Course instructor name
* **\[@INSTRUCTOR]** — Social media handle
* **\[DESIGNATION]** — Instructor title/role

**Branding:**

* **\[#HASHTAG]** — Social media hashtag

**Upsell:**

* **\[UPSELL/NEWCOURSE/SITE/NEW PROGRAM]** — Next product offering

**Footer:**

* **\[mailing\_address]** — Physical mailing address *(purple background)*
* **\[unsubscribe\_link]** — Unsubscribe URL *(purple background)*

### **🎨 VISUAL DESIGN ELEMENTS**

#### **Color Coding**

* **Purple:** Headers, active elements, buttons, toggles, tooltips, merge tags
* **Green:** "Live" status badges
* **Orange/Red:** "Paused" status badges, delete icons
* **Grey:** Input fields, inactive buttons, card backgrounds
* **Light Blue/Cyan:** Filters section background

#### **Status Badges**

* **Live:** Green background, white text
* **Paused:** Orange/red background, white text

#### **Icons \& Symbols**

* ⚡ **Lightning bolt:** Trigger indicator
* ✉️ **Envelope:** Email action indicator
* 📋 **Clone icon:** Duplicate action
* 🗑️ **Trash icon:** Delete action/rule
* ⏱️ **Clock icon:** Timing indicator
* (?) **Question mark:** Info/help tooltip trigger
* ⦿ **Filled circle:** Selected radio button
* ○ **Empty circle:** Unselected radio button
* ✓ **Checkmark:** Selected dropdown option

#### **Interactive Elements**

* **Dropdown buttons:** Down arrow indicator
* **Toggle switches:** Purple when active, grey when inactive
* **Hover tooltips:** Purple background, white text
* **Expand/collapse chevrons:** Down/up arrows on rule cards

#### **Layout Structure**

* **Two-panel layout:**

  * **Left Panel (40%):** Rule cards list
  * **Right Panel (60%):** Action editor
* **Rule cards:** Expandable/collapsible accordion style
* **Action cards:** Nested within rule cards, with timing and status information
* **Editor sections:** Vertically stacked form fields

* **Rule titles:** Purple text, bold
* **Section labels:** Regular weight, dark grey
* **Placeholder text:** Light grey
* **Helper text:** Smaller font size

### **📊 EMAIL SEQUENCE STRATEGY**

#### **Four Email Types in Documentation**

**1. Access Instructions Email**

* **Trigger:** OnEnroll
* **Timing:** Day 0 (immediately)
* **Purpose:** Welcome and course access
* **Focus:** Access details, expectations, course overview

**2. Get them to revisit the Course again**

* **Trigger:** OnEnroll
* **Timing:** Day 1 (24 hours later)
* **Purpose:** Re-engagement and urgency
* **Focus:** Completion statistics, FOMO, action deadline

**3. Drip Notification - Section Release**

* **Trigger:** OnEnroll
* **Timing:** Day 2
* **Purpose:** Content release with activities
* **Focus:** New content availability, homework, social engagement

**4. Course Completion**

* **Trigger:** OnCourseComplete
* **Timing:** Day 0 (immediately)
* **Purpose:** Congratulations and upsell
* **Focus:** Achievement celebration, social sharing, next product offer

#### **User Management Capabilities**

✓ Add individual users manually

✓ Bulk actions for multiple users  
✓ Export user data to CSV

✓ Email users directly from interface

#### **Filter Capabilities**

✓ Course enrollment status (default: "Enrolled in course")  
✓ Completion tracking  
✓ Live class attendance  
✓ Webinar registration  
✓ Email engagement (opens, clicks, bounces)  
✓ Location-based filtering  
✓ Tag-based segmentation  
✓ Custom field filtering  
✓ Multiple filter combinations with AND logic

#### **Integration Points**

✓ Connects course-level student management to site-wide people management  
✓ Filters default to showing students enrolled in the specific course  
✓ Access to full site user database from course context  
✓ Bulk enrollment/unenrollment capabilities  
✓ Tag management for course-specific segmentation

### **🔄 WORKFLOW \& BEHAVIOR**

#### **Navigation Flow**

**From Course to Students:**

1. Navigate to Courses from main menu
2. Select individual course (e.g., "Quick Start Guide")
3. Click Students tab
4. Redirects to Site / People / All
5. Default filter applied: "Enrolled in course"

**Key Behavior:**

* Students tab provides filtered view of site-wide people database
* Filter automatically set to show only students enrolled in current course
* Full site-level user management capabilities available within course context

#### **Default State**

* **Filter:** "Enrolled in course" pre-selected
* **View:** Table showing course-enrolled students only
* **Columns:** Name, Email, Joined on, Role, Tags, Actions
* **Pagination:** 15 results per page

### **🔄 WORKFLOW \& BEHAVIOR**

#### **Create Bundle Workflow**

1. Navigate to Courses → Bundles
2. Click "Create New Bundle" card
3. Redirects to Bundle Details tab
4. Fill in bundle information (name, subtitle, summary)
5. Customize URL slug (auto-generated from name)
6. Select instructor(s) from dropdown
7. Upload custom thumbnail (optional)
8. Configure visibility/access settings via checkboxes
9. Enable/disable automation and drip content
10. Toggle rating display (optional)
11. Preview updates in real-time on right side
12. Click "Create Bundle" to save
13. Add contents in Contents tab
14. Configure pricing in Pricing tab
15. Publish bundle when ready

#### **Edit Bundle Workflow**

1. Navigate to Courses → Bundles
2. Click on existing bundle card
3. Or: Click three-dot menu → select "Edit"
4. Bundle Details tab loads with saved data
5. Modify any fields as needed
6. Preview updates in real-time
7. Click "Update" to save changes

#### **Clone Bundle Workflow**

1. Navigate to Courses → Bundles
2. Click three-dot menu on bundle card
3. Select "Clone"
4. Duplicate bundle is created
5. Edit cloned bundle as needed

#### **Delete Bundle Workflow**

1. Navigate to Courses → Bundles
2. Click three-dot menu on bundle card
3. Select "Delete"
4. Confirm deletion (likely)
5. Bundle is removed from list

#### **Search Functionality**

* Real-time search as user types
* Filters bundles by name
* Updates grid display dynamically

#### **Default States**

* **New Bundle:** All fields empty except automation checkbox (checked)
* **Existing Bundle:** All fields populated with saved data
* **Instructor:** Default to creator or selectable from list
* **URL Slug:** Auto-generated from bundle name, editable
* **Thumbnail:** Grey placeholder icon until custom image uploaded
* **Rating:** Hidden by default

**🎨 VISUAL DESIGN ELEMENTS**

**Colour Scheme**

* **Purple:** Active tab underline, plan type labels, button outlines, active step indicator
* **Light Purple:** Card borders, button backgrounds
* **White:** Page background, card backgrounds
* **Grey:** Inactive text, illustrations
* **Yellow/Orange/Blue:** Illustration accent colours

**Layout Structure**

* Empty state: Centred content with illustration and button
* Add Plan: Full-width layout with step indicator at top
* Plan cards: Four equal-width cards in horizontal row
* Cards: Equal height and width, evenly spaced

**Typography**

* Page header: Large, bold ("Add Plan")
* Step indicator: Regular weight, numbered steps
* Plan names: Uppercase, purple, medium weight
* Plan descriptions: Regular weight, grey, smaller font
* Empty state message: Large, bold for main text, regular for subtext

**Interactive Elements**

* Add New Plan button: Clickable, opens plan creation flow
* Plan type cards: Selectable cards, click to choose plan type
* Step indicator: Shows current step in workflow
* Previous/Next buttons: Navigation between steps

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

**⚙️ BOTTOM ACTION BUTTONS**

**Position:** Bottom right of page

**Previous Button**

* Label: "Previous"
* Colour: Light purple background
* Position: Left button
* Function: Go back to previous step

**Next Button**

* Label: "Next"
* Colour: Purple background (darker than Previous)
* Position: Right button
* Function: Proceed to next step after selecting plan type

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

**🔑 KEY FEATURES**

**Pricing Plan Types** ✓ Free (no cost) ✓ One Time Purchase (single payment) ✓ Subscription (recurring billing - monthly or annual) ✓ Payment Plan (instalments - fixed number of payments)

**Plan Management** ✓ Add new pricing plans ✓ Choose from multiple plan types ✓ Step-by-step plan creation workflow ✓ Visual plan type selection with illustrations ✓ Clear descriptions for each plan type

**User Interface** ✓ Empty state guidance ✓ Visual step indicator ✓ Clear call-to-action buttons ✓ Illustrative icons for plan types ✓ Navigation between steps

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

**🔄 WORKFLOW \& BEHAVIOUR**

**Add First Pricing Plan Workflow**

1. Navigate to Pricing tab
2. See empty state: "No pricing plans added yet"
3. Click "Add New Plan" button
4. Redirects to Add Plan interface
5. Step 1: Choose Type screen displays
6. Four plan type cards appear
7. Select desired plan type by clicking card
8. Click "Next" button
9. Proceeds to Step 2: Add (configure plan details)

**Plan Type Selection**

* Click on any of the four plan type cards
* Selected card highlights/indicates selection
* "Next" button becomes active
* Click "Next" to proceed to configuration
* Click "Previous" to return (if applicable)

**Default State**

* Empty state shows when no plans exist
* Step 1 is active when entering Add Plan flow
* No plan type pre-selected
* Next button available after selection

**EMAIL TEMPLATE 1: ACCESS INSTRUCTIONS EMAIL**

**Instructional Block:**

\*\*\*\* Read \& Delete this block \*\*\*\*

* What is it? - This is Access Instructions Email Script. Drip ASAP.
* We are going to tell them how to access the course and what can they expect during the class.
* Do NOT set this email to Live until you edit it and make it yours.
* Edit/Delete the mail as you appropriate for your needs.
* Insert content in the short names in square brackets
* Also make sure to edit the links on this email
* Leave something in each mail for them to look forward to your next mail.

\*\*\*\* Read \& Delete this block \*\*\*\*

**Email Body:**

Hi \[first\_name],

Thank you for enrolling into my Course - \[COURSE/SITE/PROGRAM].

Today we start our 8 Week Journey to \[RESULTS].

I have packed so much into this 6 Hour course, so Please stay committed to finishing the course.

In this course I will covering:

* Section 1 : \[CONTENTS] - Available now.

**Footer:**

Mailing Address: \[mailing\_address]

If you no longer wish to receive our emails: click \[unsubscribe\_link]

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

**EMAIL TEMPLATE 2: GET THEM TO REVISIT THE COURSE AGAIN: DRIP DAY 2**

**Mail Subject:** It's been 24 hours.

**Instructional Block:**

\*\*\*\* Read \& Delete this block \*\*\*\*

* What is it? - Get them to revisit the Course again Email Script - Drip: Day 2
* This is a way to draw them back into start/resuming the Course again as most people do not the start the course. Ie this is a way to get their attention again.
* Do NOT set this email to Live until you edit it and make it yours.
* Edit/Delete the mail as you appropriate for your needs.
* Also make sure to edit the links on this email

\*\*\*\* Read \& Delete this block \*\*\*\*

**Email Body:**

Hi \[first\_name],

I know it's only been 24 hours.

Did you know that 50% of students complete Section #1 within the first 24 hours - Have You?

Also, Did you know that those who move QUICKLY on this course get the best results out of this training?.

We want to help you get the BEST results you can get, so if you haven't already, commit to finishing Section #1 by end of today.

Section Name: \[SECTION #1]

In this section, I will be covering:

* LESSON #1: \[SECRET/REVEAL1]
* LESSON #2: \[SECRET/REVEAL2]
* LESSON #3: \[SECRET/REVEAL3]

PLEASE do NOT ignore Section #1, as the information in Section 1 will be the prerequisite for entire course.

I know we all get busy with life, but please set aside 30 minutes TODAY to finish this section #1 so that you are ready for the release of Section #2 in two days.

I'll be back tomorrow with even more information on \[NEXT SECTION]. For now, goto Section #1 and finish that.

See you there,

\[INSTRUCTOR NAME]

\[DESIGNATION]

\[#HASHTAG]

**Footer:**

Mailing Address: \[mailing\_address]

If you no longer wish to receive our emails: click \[unsubscribe\_link]

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

**From Name:** Rakesh from Zenler

**From Email:** support@zenler.com

**Instructional Block:**

Hi \[first\_name],

SECTION #2 ACTIVITIES:

ACTIVITY #2: Tweet \[@INSTRUCTOR] your number one takeaway from this section. Don't forget to add # \[HASHTAG] so we know it's from you in this course.

ACTIVITY #3: Post your Commitment to everyone on social media(Facebook/Twitter/Instagram) to declare that you are going to complete it on time. This is the best way to make a commitment and accountability. And once again, Don't forget to add #\[HASHTAG] so we know it's from you in this course.

Start SECTION #1 HERE.

Talk soon,

\[INSTRUCTOR NAME]

\[DESIGNATION]

\[#HASHTAG]

Label: "Applicable to" Dropdown: "Specific Products" / "All Products"

**Dropdown Options:**

* All Products
* All Courses
* All Live Class
* All Live Webinars
* ✓ **Specific Products** (selected, highlighted in blue)

**Helper Text:** Select "All Products" if the coupon has to be applicable for all courses/bundles/live elements. Select "Specific Products" if coupon has to be applicable to specific course/bundle/live element. If the discount type chosen is "amount", you cannot apply the coupon to "All Products".

**Select Products Button**

Label: "Select Products" Appears when "Specific Products" is selected

**Currency Note:** Select the course/bundle/live element for which the discount is to be applied. If you have chosen Discount type as "Amount", the courses selected here should be of same currency.

**Redemption Limit Field**

Label: "Redemption Limit" Placeholder: "limit" Helper Text: "Maximum number of times coupon can be used. Leave blank for unlimited use." Input Type: Number field

**Expiry Field**

Label: "Expiry" Input Type: Date picker (calendar icon) Helper Text: "Coupon valid until the end of the chosen date."

📖 SAMPLE SVG CONTENT

**Section: Notes:**

**Heading** Notes:

**Subheading** How to create an svg file

**Technical Requirements (displayed in red text):** `<svg>` tag width should be 1000px and height should be 706px

**Section: Variables**

**Heading** Variables

**Description** Variables(merge tags) used are:

**Variable List:**

* {{ $schoolname }} : Will populate the school name
* {{ $studentname }} : Will populate the student name
* {{ $course }} : Will populate course name
* {{ $courseCompletionDate }} : will put certification completion date
* {{ $certificateID }} : will put certificate ID
* {{ $certificateURL }} : will put certificate URL

**Section: Fonts**

**Heading** Fonts

**Warning (displayed in red text):** Only three custom fonts are supported currently.

**Supported Fonts:**

* AlexBrush-Regular
* Pacifico
* blazium

**Font Usage Examples:**

* You can use a text like `<text font-family="AlexBrush-Regular">Student</text>` in svg
* You can use a text like `<text font-family="Pacifico">Student</text>` in svg
* You can use a text like `<text font-family="blazium">Student</text>` in svg

* Radio button options:

  * **Public** (selected)

    * Description: "Visible to all members of the community site (if the site is public, non-members can also view it). Anyone logged into the community site can directly join this community."
  * **Private**

    * Description: "Only members can view who's in the communities and what they post."
  * **Secret**

    * Description: "Visible only to joined members. Only invited users or those added by the admin can view this community."

You have successfully booked a spot on the Live Webinar by {webinar\_host}

Topic : {webinar\_topic} When : {webinar\_scheduled\_time} Join Link : {webinar\_join\_link}

Please add this to your Calendar. See you on the Webinar.

Thanks {webinar\_host}"

Reminder Email (1 Hour): "Hi {first\_name}

This is a reminder that our webinar {webinar\_topic} will take place within 1 hour.

Use the link below to join at the specified date \& time. {webinar\_join\_link}

We will see you on webinar.

Thanks {webinar\_host}"

Reminder Email (1 Day): "Hi {first\_name}

We will see you on webinar.

Thanks {webinar\_host}"

**CLOUD RECORDING TAB - COMPLETE DOCUMENTATION**

**Page Header:**

Webinar Title: "Office Hours - Your Regular Heartbeat to Zenler"

Breadcrumb: "Live / Live Interactive Webinar / Office Hours - Your Regular Heartbeat to Zenler"

Webinar Thumbnail:

* Purple/blue gradient background
* "OFFICE HOURS" text
* Person illustration
* Zenler branding

**Tab Navigation:**

* Webinar Details
* Pages
* Pricing
* Automation
* Registrants
* Cloud Recording (active)

**Results Counter:**

* Display text: "Showing 8 Results"
* Located top left of table

**Cloud Recording Table**

**Table Columns:**

Column 1 - #:

* Column header: "#"
* Sequential numbering of recordings
* Values: 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8

Column 2 - Time:

* Column header: "Time"
* Displays date and time of recording
* Format: YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM AM/PM
* Example values:

  * 2026-02-12 01:00 PM
  * 2026-02-21 11:00 AM
  * 2026-02-26 03:00 PM
  * 2026-03-05 01:00 PM
  * 2026-03-11 03:00 PM
  * 2026-03-18 03:05 PM
  * 2026-03-26 01:00 PM
  * 2026-04-09 12:00 PM

Column 3 - Recording:

* Column header: "Recording"
* Displays full Zenler Zoom recording URL
* Format: [https://zenler.zoom.us/rec/share/\[unique\_recording\_id](https://zenler.zoom.us/rec/share/%5Bunique_recording_id)]
* URLs are full-length shareable links
* Example format:

  * [https://zenler.zoom.us/rec/share/boxvaTfwlkR\_LP3MSSD0KQOJk4RQGMHGtailNxnrSiNPHXoUT8cKLlMLh2h3v-T6BJJGQGBzKAgtyGQAqbM](https://zenler.zoom.us/rec/share/boxvaTfwlkR_LP3MSSD0KQOJk4RQGMHGtailNxnrSiNPHXoUT8cKLlMLh2h3v-T6BJJGQGBzKAgtyGQAqbM)

Column 4 - Actions:

* Column header: "Actions"
* Three action icons per recording row:

  * Link/Copy icon (chain link) - Copy recording URL
  * Download icon (down arrow with line) - Download recording
  * Delete icon (trash bin) - Delete recording
* Icons displayed in horizontal row
* Light grey background on icon buttons

**Table Features:**

* Full width table layout
* White background rows
* Alternating row colours for readability
* Responsive column widths
* Recording column takes majority of width to display full URLs
* Clean, minimal design
* Horizontal scrolling for long URLs if needed

**Recording URL Structure:** All recordings follow the Zenler Zoom format:

* Base URL: [https://zenler.zoom.us/rec/share/](https://zenler.zoom.us/rec/share/)
* Followed by unique recording identifier
* URLs are directly shareable and playable

**Note:** The Cloud Recording tab displays all recorded sessions from the Live Interactive Webinar. For recurring webinars, each session creates a separate recording entry. The recordings are automatically generated when "Automatic Cloud Recording" is enabled in the webinar settings. Each recording can be copied, downloaded, or deleted individually. The recordings are hosted on Zenler's Zoom integration and remain accessible until manually deleted.

* Clickable timestamps (likely jumps to that point in video)

* ## White background with clear text hierarchy

**Page Layout:**

Two-column layout:

* Left column: Video player (approximately 65-70% width)
* Right column: Transcript panel (approximately 30-35% width)
* Responsive design
* Clean white background
* Minimal distraction from content

**Note:** This is the public playback view that appears when someone accesses a cloud recording URL. It provides a complete viewing experience with integrated video playback, transcript, and chat messages. The interface is optimised for both live viewing and replay consumption, with features like speed control, captions, and searchable transcripts enhancing accessibility and usability.

# **SITE SECTION - COMPLETE DOCUMENTATION**

## **SITE → SITE SETTINGS**

### **Navigation Structure**

**Left Sidebar Menu:**

* Site Settings
* Branding
* Home Page
* Pages
* Blog
* Language

* Tax

* Affiliate Programs

* API v2.0
* Partner Program

\---

### **SITE SETTINGS - 11 TABS**

#### **1. SITE DETAILS TAB**

**Site Name:**

* Text input field
* Character counter displayed
* Site name appears throughout platform
* Visible in browser tabs, navigation, and branding

**Site URL:**

* Format: https:// + \[subdomain] + .newzenler.com
* Editable subdomain field
* Fixed suffix: .newzenler.com
* This is the default site URL
* Cannot be changed to custom domain in this field

**Account Owner Permissions:**

* Controls who can manage site settings
* Permission management for site administration
* Defines admin access levels

\---

#### **2. CUSTOM DOMAIN TAB**

**Connect Your Own Domain:**

* Option to use custom domain instead of subdomain
* URL prefix: https://
* Example format provided
* Instructions for domain connection
* Requires DNS configuration
* Custom domain setup guidance

\---

#### **3. GDPR SETTINGS TAB**

**GDPR Toggle:**

* ON/OFF switch
* Enables GDPR compliance features
* Controls data protection settings
* Required for EU users

**EU Marketing Consent Text:**

* Text input field
* Customizable consent message for EU users
* Displays during registration/signup
* Legal compliance requirement

**Cookie Message:**

* Text input for cookie notification
* Displays to visitors regarding cookie usage
* Appears as banner or popup
* Dismissible by users

**Cookie Policy:**

* Complete cookie policy reference
* Link to full policy details
* Legal documentation requirement
* Explains data collection practices

\---

#### **4. ADVANCED SETTINGS TAB**

**Terms \& Conditions:**

* Checkbox toggle with tooltip
* Enable/disable Terms \& Conditions requirement
* Info icon with additional details
* Forces users to accept terms during registration
* Optional but recommended

\---

#### **5. INVOICE TAB**

**Download Invoice:**

* Toggle switch
* Enable/disable invoice download for customers
* Allows customers to access purchase receipts
* Useful for business customers

**VAT ID:**

* Text input field
* For businesses with VAT registration
* Appears on invoices
* EU tax compliance

**Additional Text:**

* Text area
* Custom text to appear on invoices
* Can include company details, disclaimers, or notes
* Appears at bottom of invoice

\---

#### **6. ADDRESS TAB**

**Business Details Form:**

All fields marked with asterisk (\*) are required:

**Name**\*

* Business/Organization name
* Text input field
* Required field
* Appears on invoices and legal documents

**Address**\*

* Street address
* Text input field
* Required field
* Physical business location

**City**\*

* City/Town
* Text input field
* Required field

**State**\*

* State/Province/Region
* Text input field
* Required field

**Zip Code**\*

* Postal code
* Text input field
* Required field

**Country**\*

* Dropdown selector
* Required field
* Full country list available

**Purpose:**

* Billing address
* Invoice generation
* Legal documentation
* Tax compliance

\---

#### **7. EMAILS TAB**

**Welcome Emails:**

* Toggle switch
* Enable/disable automated welcome emails to new users
* Sent upon registration/signup
* Can be customized in email templates section

**Cart Abandonment:**

* Toggle switch
* Enable/disable abandoned cart emails
* **Timing dropdown options:**

  * 1 hour
  * 6 hours
  * 12 hours
  * 24 hours
* Automated reminder emails for abandoned carts
* Helps recover lost sales

**Double Opt-in:**

* Toggle switch
* Require email confirmation for new subscribers
* Sends confirmation email before adding to list
* Ensures valid email addresses
* GDPR compliance feature

\---

#### **8. GLOBAL INCLUDES TAB**

**Code Injection Sections:**

**All Pages:**

* `<head>` section text area
* Accepts script and style tags only
* Code applies to all site pages
* Useful for:

  * Google Analytics
  * Facebook Pixel
  * Custom CSS
  * Third-party tracking codes
  * Fonts from external CDNs

**Course Player:**

* `<head>` section text area
* Accepts script and style tags only
* Code applies only within course player
* Isolated from main site
* Useful for:

  * Course-specific analytics
  * Custom player styling
  * Learning tracking scripts

**Purpose:**

* Custom tracking codes
* Analytics scripts
* Custom CSS styles
* Third-party integrations
* No-code customization

\---

#### **9. TIME ZONE TAB**

**Time Zone Selector:**

* Dropdown menu
* Placeholder: "Select Time Zone"
* Full list of world timezones
* Sets default timezone for entire site
* Affects:

  * Scheduling (webinars, classes)
  * Timestamps on content
  * Reporting dates/times
  * Email send times
  * Automation triggers

\---

#### **10. SITEMAP TAB**

**Sitemap URL:**

* Read-only text field
* Displays full sitemap URL
* Format: https://\[yourdomain]/sitemap.xml
* Copy button for easy copying
* Submit to search engines

**Recreate Sitemap:**

* Button to regenerate sitemap
* Updates sitemap with current pages
* Important after:

  * Adding new pages
  * Removing pages
  * Publishing blog posts
  * Creating new courses
  * Structural site changes
* SEO best practice

\---

#### **11. ROBOTS TAB**

**Robots.txt Content:**

* Large text area
* Edit robots.txt file content directly
* Example/placeholder text provided
* Controls search engine crawler behavior
* Directives include:

  * User-agent (which bots)
  * Disallow (blocked URLs)
  * Allow (permitted URLs)
  * Sitemap location
* SEO and indexing control

\---

## **SITE → BRANDING**

### **LOGO SECTION**

**Logo Upload:**

* Upload area for site logo
* Square display format recommended
* Appears top-left navigation throughout site
* Visible on:

  * All site pages
  * Header navigation
  * Email templates
  * Student dashboard
* File formats: PNG, JPG, SVG
* Recommended size: 200x200px minimum
* Transparent background recommended for PNG

### **FAVICON SECTION**

**Favicon Upload:**

* Smaller square upload area
* Default: Purple/blue gradient "Z" icon
* Appears in:

  * Browser tabs
  * Bookmark lists
  * Browser history
  * Mobile home screen shortcuts
* File format: ICO, PNG
* Recommended size: 32x32px or 64x64px
* Square aspect ratio required

### **FONT FAMILY SECTION**

**Pre-configured Font Combinations:**

* Grid layout: 5 columns × 4 rows = 20 combinations
* Each shows heading + body font pairing
* Examples include:

  * Open Sans / Roboto
  * Playfair Display / Lato
  * Oswald / Raleway
  * Montserrat / Lato
  * Merriweather / Open Sans
  * Lora / Source Sans Pro
  * Poppins / Inter
  * Roboto / Open Sans
  * Ubuntu / Lato
  * Nunito / Source Sans Pro
  * PT Sans / PT Serif
  * Work Sans / Crimson Text
  * Karla / Merriweather
* Click to select combination
* Applies to entire site instantly
* Professional, tested pairings

**Browse Font Button:**

* Access additional Google Fonts
* Extended font library (900+ fonts)
* Searchable font list
* Preview available
* Free to use

\---

### **CUSTOM FONT SECTION**

**Upload Custom Fonts:**

* Upload button
* Help link for instructions
* Supports web font formats:

  * WOFF
  * WOFF2
  * TTF
  * OTF
* Upload your brand fonts
* License compliance required

**Custom Fonts Table:**

**Columns:**

* **Title** - Display name for font
* **Font Family** - Technical font name (used in CSS)
* **Preview** - Sample text display showing font
* **Actions** - Eye icon (preview), Trash icon (delete)

**Example Fonts:**

* "Growtesk - Title" (armin\_groteskultrabold)
* "BODY BRAND" (pp\_object\_sansregular)

**Font Management:**

* Add multiple custom fonts
* Preview before applying
* Delete unused fonts
* Organize by purpose (heading, body, accent)

### **SET YOUR OWN FONT PAIR**

**Heading Font:**

* Dropdown selector
* Choose font for all headings (H1-H6)
* Includes:

  * Google Fonts library
  * Uploaded custom fonts
  * System fonts
* Searchable list

**Heading Font Style:**

* Dropdown selector
* **Options (availability depends on selected font):**

  * Thin
  * Thin Italic
  * Light
  * Light Italic
  * Regular
  * Regular Italic
  * Medium
  * Medium Italic
  * Bold
  * Bold Italic
  * Black
  * Black Italic
* Not all fonts have all styles
* Controls font weight and slant

**Body Font:**

* Dropdown selector
* Choose font for all body text
* Includes:

  * Google Fonts library
  * Uploaded custom fonts
  * System fonts
* Searchable list
* Can be same or different from heading

**Body Font Style:**

* Dropdown selector
* Same style options as heading
* Typically Regular or Light for readability
* Controls font weight and slant

**Save Button:**

* Apply custom font pairing
* Changes apply site-wide immediately
* Affects all text unless overridden in page builder

**Best Practices:**

* Use maximum 2-3 fonts site-wide
* Pair serif with sans-serif for contrast
* Ensure readability for body text
* Test on multiple devices

* Typically:

  * About page
  * Marketing content
  * Sign-up encouragement

**Logged In:**

* Dropdown selector
* Label: "Logged In"
* Select page for authenticated users
* Shows when user signed in
* Typically:

  * Student dashboard
  * My courses page
  * Community hub
  * Member area

**Save Changes Button:**

* Blue button
* Apply homepage selections
* Changes take effect immediately
* No page refresh required

**Use Case Examples:**

**E-commerce/Course Site:**

* Logged Out: Marketing landing page
* Logged In: Student dashboard

**Community/Membership:**

* Logged Out: About/join page
* Logged In: Community feed

**Mixed Content:**

* Logged Out: Sales page
* Logged In: Course library

**Benefits:**

* Personalized user experience
* Reduces friction for members
* Increases engagement
* Better conversion optimization
* Separate marketing from member content

## **SITE → PAGES**

### **PAGES MANAGEMENT INTERFACE**

**Page Title:** Pages

**Top Controls:**

**Add Page Button:**

* Location: Top right
* Blue button
* Text: "Add Page"
* Opens Add Page modal
* Quick page creation

**Search Field:**

* Location: Top left/center
* Placeholder: "Find page"
* Search by page title
* Real-time filtering
* Case-insensitive search

\---

### **PAGES TABLE**

**Table Layout:**

* Three columns
* Responsive design
* Sortable (potentially)
* Paginated for large lists

**Columns:**

#### **1. THUMBNAIL**

**Display:**

* Preview image of page
* Small thumbnail size (approximately 100-150px wide)
* Visual page identification
* Screenshot of actual page
* Helps locate pages quickly

**Content Shown:**

* Page header
* First section visible
* General layout preview
* Actual page styling

#### **2. PAGE DETAILS**

**Page Title:**

* Large, bold text
* Primary identifier
* Clickable link to edit page
* Search matches highlighted

**Full URL:**

* Format: [https://links.zenler.com/\[page-slug](https://links.zenler.com/%5Bpage-slug)]
* Complete accessible URL
* Copyable text
* Shows subdomain
* Displays slug/path

**Status Badge:**

**Published Status:**

* Green badge
* Text: "Published"
* Page is live and accessible
* Visible to appropriate users (based on privacy settings)

**Unpublished Status:**

* Red badge
* Text: "Unpublished"
* Page is draft/hidden
* Not accessible to public
* Only visible to admins

#### **3. ACTIONS**

**Action Icons:** (Displayed horizontally for each page)

**Eye Icon (Preview):**

* Preview page
* Opens in new tab
* Shows page as users see it
* No edit mode
* Quick check before publishing

**Pencil Icon (Edit):**

* Edit page
* Opens page builder
* Modify content and settings
* Full editing access

**Link Icon (Copy URL):**

* Copy page URL to clipboard
* One-click copy
* Share link easily
* Useful for marketing

**Duplicate Icon (Clone):**

* Clone/duplicate page
* Creates exact copy
* New page with "-copy" suffix
* Preserves all content and settings
* Quick template creation

**Trash Icon (Delete):**

* Delete page
* Confirmation prompt shown
* Permanent deletion
* Cannot be undone (usually)
* Removes from site completely

\---

### **ADD PAGE INTERFACE**

**Modal Title:** Add Page

**Page Title:**

* Text input field
* Placeholder: "Page title"
* Required field (marked with \*)
* Controls page name in admin
* Used for navigation
* Auto-generates URL slug

**Page URL:**

* **Prefix:** [https://links.zenler.com/](https://links.zenler.com/)
* **Editable slug field**
* Auto-populated from page title
* Lowercase conversion
* Spaces become hyphens
* Special characters removed
* Can be manually edited

**Reserved URLs Note:** "Cannot use the following slugs:"

* /login
* /register
* /home
* /courses
* /terms
* /privacypolicy
* /support
* /webinar
* /mycourses
* /cookiepolicy
* /community
* /blog
* /unsubscribe

**Reason:**

* System pages
* Functionality conflicts
* SEO issues
* User confusion
* Platform reserved paths

\---

**Select Template:**

**Section Title:** "Select Template"

**Three Template Options:**

#### **1. BLANK PAGE**

**Thumbnail:**

* White background
* Dotted pattern/grid
* Empty canvas visualization
* Minimal design

**Label:** "Blank Page"

**Description:**

* No pre-configured content
* Start from scratch
* Complete creative freedom
* Build custom layout
* Use blocks and elements

**Best For:**

* Custom designs
* Unique layouts
* Specific requirements
* Experienced users
* Brand-specific pages

#### **2. GENERAL TEMPLATE 1**

**Thumbnail:**

* Dark header section visible
* Three-column layout shown
* Feature cards with images
* Professional design preview

**Label:** "General Template 1"

**Pre-configured Content:**

* Hero section (dark or light variant)
* Three-column feature section
* Image blocks
* Heading blocks
* Description blocks

**Best For:**

* Service showcases
* Feature presentations
* Product overviews
* Multi-point marketing
* Professional services

#### **3. GENERAL TEMPLATE 2**

**Thumbnail:**

* White/light background
* Centered video frame visible
* Two-column layout
* Clean, minimal design

**Label:** "General Template 2"

**Pre-configured Content:**

* Two-column layout
* Text content (left)
* Video embed (right)
* Heading block
* Description block

**Best For:**

* Video landing pages
* Course introductions
* Product demonstrations
* Webinar promotions
* Single-message pages

\---

**Publish This Page:**

* Checkbox
* Label: "Publish This Page"
* Helper text: "Only published pages will be visible in the site"
* **Unchecked:** Page saved as draft/unpublished
* **Checked:** Page goes live immediately
* Affects visibility to non-admin users

\---

**Page Privacy:**

**Section Title:** "Page Privacy"

**Radio Button Options:**

**Option 1 (Default):**

* Radio button
* Label: "Page is visible for All users"
* Page accessible to everyone
* No login required
* Public page
* Indexed by search engines (if settings allow)

**Option 2:**

* Radio button
* Label: "Page is visible Only for Logged in users"
* Page requires authentication
* Login wall
* Members-only content
* Not indexed by search engines
* Protected content

\---

**Action Buttons:**

**Cancel:**

* Grey/white button
* Text: "Cancel"
* Closes modal
* No changes saved
* Returns to Pages list

**Add:**

* Blue button
* Text: "Add"
* Creates new page
* Saves all settings
* Opens page builder (or returns to list)
* Validation required

## **SITE → PAGES → PAGE BUILDER**

### **TOP TOOLBAR**

**Toolbar Location:** Top of page builder interface **Background:** Dark grey/black **Fixed Position:** Stays visible while scrolling

\---

#### **LEFT SIDE TOOLS**

**1. BLOCKS**

* **Icon:** Four squares in grid pattern (2×2)
* **Function:** Opens blocks library
* **Content:** Pre-built content blocks
* **Examples:** Text, image, video, button, form, divider

**2. ELEMENTS**

* **Icon:** Layered shapes/elements
* **Function:** Opens elements library
* **Content:** Design elements and widgets
* **Examples:** Icons, shapes, spacers, social

**3. POPUP**

* **Icon:** Window/modal with plus symbol
* **Function:** Create popups/modals
* **Content:** Exit intent, timed, click-triggered popups
* **Use:** Lead capture, announcements, promotions

**4. ZENLER AI**

* **Icon:** Sparkle/star symbol
* **Badge:** Green "AI" label
* **Function:** AI-powered content assistance
* **Features:**

  * Content generation
  * Text improvement
  * SEO optimization
  * Copywriting help

\---

#### **CENTER SECTION**

**PAGE SELECTOR**

* **Type:** Dropdown menu
* **Display:** Current page name
* **Function:** Switch between pages in multi-page editor
* **Arrow:** Dropdown indicator
* **Quick Navigation:** Jump between pages without leaving builder

\---

#### **RIGHT SIDE TOOLS**

**5. UNDO**

* **Icon:** Curved arrow pointing left
* **Function:** Reverse last action
* **Keyboard Shortcut:** Likely Ctrl+Z or Cmd+Z
* **Unlimited:** Multiple undo steps

**6. REDO**

* **Icon:** Curved arrow pointing right
* **Function:** Reapply undone action
* **Keyboard Shortcut:** Likely Ctrl+Y or Cmd+Shift+Z
* **Works:** Only after undo

**7. GRID/LAYOUT**

* **Icon:** Grid pattern
* **Function:** Toggle grid view
* **Purpose:**

  * Visual alignment
  * Spacing reference
  * Design precision
  * Snap-to-grid

**8. DEVICE PREVIEW**

* **Type:** Dropdown menu
* **Icon:** Device symbols
* **Options:**

  * Desktop (monitor icon)
  * Tablet (tablet icon)
  * Mobile (phone icon)
* **Function:** Preview responsive design
* **Live:** Real-time device simulation

**9. SAVE**

* **Button Style:** Grey button
* **Icon:** Floppy disk icon
* **Text:** "Save"
* **Function:** Save all changes
* **Auto-save:** May have auto-save in background
* **Indicator:** Status shown (saved/unsaved)

**10. PREVIEW**

* **Button Style:** White/light button
* **Icon:** Eye icon
* **Text:** "Preview"
* **Function:** Preview page as visitor
* **New Tab:** Opens in new window
* **No Edit:** View-only mode

**11. MORE OPTIONS**

* **Icon:** Three dots (kebab menu)
* **Type:** Dropdown menu
* **Function:** Additional settings and tools

\---

### **MORE OPTIONS MENU**

**Menu Items:**

**1. PAGE SETTINGS**

* **Icon:** Gear/cog icon
* **Function:** Page-level settings
* **Options:**

  * Page title
  * URL slug
  * Privacy settings
  * Template selection

**2. SEO SETTINGS**

* **Icon:** Globe icon
* **Function:** Search engine optimization
* **Options:**

  * Meta title
  * Meta description
  * Keywords
  * Open Graph tags
  * Twitter cards
  * Schema markup

**3. TRACKING CODE**

* **Icon:** Code brackets <>
* **Function:** Add tracking scripts
* **Options:**

  * Page-specific tracking
  * Analytics code
  * Conversion pixels
  * Custom scripts

**4. VERSION HISTORY**

* **Icon:** Clock/history icon
* **Function:** View page versions
* **Features:**

  * Restore previous versions
  * Compare changes
  * Undo major changes
  * Audit trail

**5. EDITOR PREFERENCES**

* **Icon:** Toggle sliders icon
* **Function:** Editor settings
* **Options:**

  * Grid visibility
  * Snap settings
  * Keyboard shortcuts
  * Auto-save preferences

**6. RETURN TO SITE**

* **Icon:** Left arrow icon
* **Function:** Exit page builder
* **Destination:** Return to Pages list
* **Save Prompt:** May prompt to save unsaved changes

**Panel Location:** Right sidebar **Panel Width:** Fixed sidebar width **Scrollable:** Yes, for long content

* **Icon:** Brush/paint icon

#### **HEADER TOP ACTION BAR**

**Bar Location:** Top of settings panel **Icon Row:** Horizontal action icons

**Icons:**

**Lock Icon:**

* Function: Save as template
* Purpose: Save header configuration for reuse
* Creates: Reusable header template
* Library: Available in template library

**Folder Icon:**

* Function: Save configuration
* Purpose: Save current settings
* Quick: One-click save
* Preserves: All styling and menu structure

**Eye Icon:**

* Function: Preview header
* Purpose: View as users see it
* Context: Shows header in live context
* No Edit: View-only mode

**Trash Icon:**

* Function: Delete header
* Purpose: Remove header from page
* Warning: Confirmation prompt
* Impact: Removes all header content

\---

#### **HEADER STYLE TAB**

**Section Title:** Style

**Color Fields (9 Total):**

**1. Background:**

* Colour picker dropdown
* Header background colour
* Full-width header bar
* Default: White or transparent

**2. Text:**

* Colour picker dropdown
* General header text colour
* Non-link text
* Contrast: Must be readable on background

**3. Link:**

* Colour picker dropdown
* Navigation link colour
* Menu item text
* Default state
* Stand out from regular text

**4. Link Hover:**

* Colour picker dropdown
* Link hover state colour
* Mouse hover feedback
* Visual interactivity
* Often: Underline or colour change

**5. Button:**

* Colour picker dropdown
* Header button background
* CTA buttons (Sign Up, Login)
* Brand colour typically

**6. Button Text:**

* Colour picker dropdown
* Text inside buttons
* High contrast required
* Readable on button background

**7. Button Border:**

* Colour picker dropdown
* Button outline colour
* Optional styling element
* Can match or contrast button

**8. Button Hover:**

* Colour picker dropdown
* Button hover background colour
* Mouse hover feedback
* Often: Slightly darker/lighter

**9. Button Hover Text:**

* Colour picker dropdown
* Text colour when button hovered
* Maintains readability
* Contrast with hover background

**Layout Options:**

**Full Width Toggle:**

* Toggle switch (ON/OFF)
* Label: "Full Width"

**When ON:**

* Header spans full browser width
* Edge-to-edge design
* No side margins
* Modern look

**When OFF:**

* Header respects site max-width
* Contained width
* Side margins visible
* Boxed layout

\---

**Action Buttons:**

**Reset Button:**

* Blue/grey button
* Text: "Reset"
* Function: Restore default header colours only
* Scope: Color fields only
* Preserves: Menu structure

**Reset Block Button:**

* Blue/grey button
* Text: "Reset Block"
* Function: Reset entire header configuration
* Scope: All settings (colors, menu, layout)
* Warning: May prompt for confirmation
* Destructive: Returns to default state

\---

#### **HEADER MENU TAB**

**Section Title:** Menu

**Menu Links Section:**

**List Display:**

* Vertical list of menu items
* Each item on separate row
* Drag handles for reordering
* Nested structure visible (sub-menus indented)

**Each Menu Item Shows:**

**Drag Handle:**

* Icon: Six dots or lines (⋮⋮)
* Function: Reorder menu items
* Drag: Up/down to change position
* Visual: Cursor changes on hover

**Link Text:**

* Menu item display text
* What users see
* Editable inline (potentially)
* Primary identifier

**Plus Icon (Add Sub-menu):**

* Icon: Plus symbol (+)
* Function: Add sub-menu item
* Creates: Dropdown/nested menu
* Hierarchy: Builds menu structure

**Pencil Icon (Edit):**

* Icon: Pencil/edit symbol
* Function: Edit menu item
* Opens: Edit menu item form
* Modify: Text, link, visibility

**Trash Icon (Delete):**

* Icon: Trash bin/delete symbol
* Function: Delete menu item
* Warning: Confirmation prompt
* Permanent: Removes item

\---

**Add New Menu Button:**

* Button style: Blue/accent colour
* Text: "Add New Menu" or "Add New Link"
* Location: Below menu items list
* Function: Create new top-level menu item
* Opens: Add menu item form

\---

**Hide Community Menu Toggle:**

* Toggle switch
* Label: "Hide Community Menu"
* Function: Show/hide community navigation
* **When ON:** Community menu hidden
* **When OFF:** Community menu visible
* Applies: If community feature enabled

\---

**Reset Block Button:**

* Blue/grey button
* Text: "Reset Block"
* Location: Bottom of menu tab
* Function: Restore default menu structure
* Warning: Confirmation prompt
* Resets: All menu items to default

\---

#### **MENU ITEM FORMS**

**FORM 1: ADD NEW MENU ITEM**

**Form Title:** Add New Menu Item (or similar)

**Fields:**

**Text:**

* Text input field
* Label: "Text"
* Placeholder: "Menu item text"
* Required: Yes
* Purpose: Link label visible to users
* Example: "Home", "About", "Courses"

**Visible to:**

* Dropdown selector
* Label: "Visible to"
* **Options:**

  * **None** - Menu item hidden
  * **Logged In** - Only authenticated users
  * **Logged Out** - Only non-authenticated users
  * **All** - Everyone (default)
* Purpose: Conditional visibility
* Use Case: Show different menus for members

**Action:**

* Dropdown selector
* Label: "Action"
* **Options:**

  * **Select an action** - Placeholder
  * **Go to Link** - Navigate to URL
  * **Dropdown** - Create sub-menu
* Purpose: Define click behavior
* Dependent: Shows/hides Link field

**Link:**

* Text input field
* Label: "Link"
* Visible: Only when "Go to Link" selected
* Format: URL (internal or external)
* Examples:

  * Internal: /about, /courses
  * External: [https://example.com](https://example.com)
* Validation: URL format

**Open in new tab:**

* Toggle switch
* Label: "Open in new tab"
* **When ON:** Link opens in new browser tab/window
* **When OFF:** Link opens in same tab
* Applies: Only to "Go to Link" action
* Best Practice: ON for external links

\---

**Action Buttons:**

**Cancel:**

* Grey/white button
* Text: "Cancel"
* Function: Close form without saving
* Returns: To menu items list

**Save:**

* Blue button
* Text: "Save"
* Function: Create new menu item
* Validation: Checks required fields
* Result: Item added to menu list

\---

**FORM 2: EDIT MENU ITEM**

**Form Title:** Edit Menu Item (or similar)

**Fields:**

* Same as Add form
* Pre-populated with existing values
* All fields editable

**Text:**

* Current menu item text shown

**Visible to:**

* Current visibility setting selected

**Action:**

* Current action selected

**Link:**

* Current URL shown (if applicable)

**Open in new tab:**

* Current setting shown

\---

**Action Buttons:**

**Cancel:**

* Grey/white button
* Text: "Cancel"
* Function: Close without saving changes
* Returns: To menu items list

**Update:**

* Blue button
* Text: "Update" (instead of "Save")
* Function: Save changes to existing item
* Validation: Checks required fields
* Result: Menu item updated in list

\---

**MODAL: ADD SUB MENU**

**Modal Title:** Add Sub Menu

**Purpose:**

* Create dropdown/nested menu item
* Child of parent menu item
* Hierarchical navigation

**Fields:**

**Text:**

* Text input field
* Label: "Text"
* Placeholder: "Sub-menu item text"
* Required: Yes
* Purpose: Sub-menu item label

**Action:**

* Dropdown selector
* Label: "Action"
* **Options:**

  * **Select an action**
  * **Go to Link**
  * **Dropdown** (create further nesting)
* Purpose: Define sub-menu click behavior

**Link:**

* Text input field
* Label: "Link"
* Visible: When "Go to Link" selected
* Format: URL (internal/external)

**Visible to:**

* Dropdown selector (potentially)
* Inherits: Parent visibility (potentially)
* Override: Custom visibility per sub-item

\---

**Action Buttons:**

**Cancel:**

* Grey/white button
* Text: "Cancel"
* Function: Close modal without adding
* Returns: To parent menu context

**Save:**

* Blue button
* Text: "Save"
* Function: Add sub-menu item
* Nesting: Creates hierarchy
* Result: Sub-menu appears under parent

  * [https://facebook.com/yourpage](https://facebook.com/yourpage)
  * [https://twitter.com/yourhandle](https://twitter.com/yourhandle)
  * [https://instagram.com/youraccount](https://instagram.com/youraccount)
  * [https://youtube.com/yourchannel](https://youtube.com/yourchannel)
* Validation: URL format

**Open in new tab:**

* Toggle switch (potentially)
* Best Practice: ON for social links
* User Experience: Keep visitors on site

\---

**Modal Buttons:**

**Cancel:**

* Close modal without adding
* Returns to icons list

**Save/Add:**

* Add icon to footer
* Saves all configuration
* Icon appears in footer icons list

\---

**Footer Menu Controls:**

**Hide Community Menu Toggle:**

* Toggle switch
* Label: "Hide Community Menu"
* Function: Show/hide community footer links
* **When ON:** Community footer hidden
* **When OFF:** Community footer visible
* Applies: If community enabled

**Reset Block Button:**

* Blue/grey button
* Text: "Reset Block"
* Location: Bottom of menu tab
* Function: Restore default footer menu
* Scope: Links and icons
* Warning: Confirmation prompt
* Result: Returns to default state

\---

### **ADD ROWS MODAL**

**Modal Title:** Rows **Modal Purpose:** Insert pre-built row layouts into page

**Close Button:**

* Icon: X symbol
* Location: Top right corner
* Function: Close modal without selection

\---

**10 Layout Templates:**

**Display Grid:** 2 rows × 5 columns

\---

#### **TOP ROW LAYOUTS**

**Layout 1: 100%**

* Visual: Single full-width block
* Description: Full width single column
* Use: Hero sections, full-width images, banners
* Structure: One column at 100%

**Layout 2: 50%-50%**

* Visual: Two equal blocks side by side
* Description: Two equal columns
* Use: Side-by-side content, feature comparison
* Structure: Two columns at 50% each

**Layout 3: 33%-66%**

* Visual: Narrow left, wide right
* Description: Left narrow, right wide
* Use: Sidebar left, main content right
* Structure: 33% left column, 66% right column

**Layout 4: 66%-33%**

* Visual: Wide left, narrow right
* Description: Left wide, right narrow
* Use: Main content left, sidebar right
* Structure: 66% left column, 33% right column

**Layout 5: 75%-25%**

* Visual: Dominant left, small right
* Description: Left dominant, right sidebar
* Use: Blog posts with sidebar, featured content
* Structure: 75% left column, 25% right column

\---

#### **BOTTOM ROW LAYOUTS**

**Layout 6: 25%-75%**

* Visual: Small left, dominant right
* Description: Left sidebar, right dominant
* Use: Navigation sidebar, main content area
* Structure: 25% left column, 75% right column

**Layout 7: 33%-33%-33%**

* Visual: Three equal blocks
* Description: Three equal columns
* Use: Feature cards, service listings, team members
* Structure: Three columns at 33% each

**Layout 8: 25%-25%-25%-25%**

* Visual: Four equal blocks
* Description: Four equal columns
* Use: Icon grids, product showcases, stats
* Structure: Four columns at 25% each

**Layout 9: 25%-25%-25%-25% (alternate)**

* Visual: Four equal blocks (variant)
* Description: Four equal columns (alternate styling)
* Use: Same as Layout 8, different internal structure
* Structure: Four columns at 25% each

**Layout 10: 50%-50% (with subdivisions)**

* Visual: Two columns, internally subdivided
* Description: Complex nested layout
* Use: Advanced layouts, mixed content types
* Structure: Two 50% columns, each with internal splits

\---

**Visual Representation:**

**Dotted Dividers:**

* Show column boundaries
* Vertical lines between columns
* Dashed/dotted style
* Visual guides

**Percentage Labels:**

* Displayed on each column
* Shows width percentage
* Helps quick identification
* Clear labeling

\---

**Interaction:**

**Click to Select:**

* Click any template
* Template inserts at cursor position
* Modal closes automatically
* No confirmation needed

**Insertion Point:**

* At cursor position in page
* Between existing rows
* At top or bottom of page
* Visual indicator before insertion

\---

### **ROW/SECTION EDITING**

#### **SELECTION INDICATORS**

**Row Level Selection:**

* **Border:** Green/teal outline
* **Toolbar:** Green background with controls
* **Icons:** Green color scheme
* **Scope:** Full-width selection
* **Indicates:** Section-level editing

**Block Level Selection:**

* **Border:** Red outline
* **Toolbar:** Red background with controls
* **Icons:** Red color scheme
* **Scope:** Content-width selection
* **Indicates:** Block-level editing

**Column Level Selection:**

* **Border:** Red outline around selected column
* **Parent Row:** Dotted grey outline
* **Indicates:** Column-specific editing
* **Nested:** Shows hierarchy

\---

**Visual Hierarchy:**

* **Green:** Row/Section level (outermost)
* **Red:** Block/Column level (inner content)
* **Dotted outline:** Parent container (not actively selected)
* **Purpose:** Clear editing context

\---

#### **ROW-LEVEL TOOLBAR (GREEN)**

**Toolbar Location:** Top of selected row **Toolbar Style:** Green/teal background, white icons **Toolbar Width:** Spans row width

\---

**LEFT SIDE CONTROLS:**

**1. Lock/Save Template Icon:**

* **Icon:** Lock/padlock symbol
* **Function:** Save row layout as reusable template
* **Tooltip:** "Save Block As Template" (on hover)
* **Purpose:**

  * Save current row configuration
  * Preserve column structure
  * Include all content
  * Reuse across pages
* **Result:** Template added to library

**2. Duplicate Icon:**

* **Icon:** Two overlapping squares
* **Function:** Clone entire row
* **Result:**

  * Creates identical copy
  * Positioned directly below original
  * Includes all content and settings
  * Maintains column structure
* **Use:** Quick replication

**3. Folder/Save Icon:**

* **Icon:** Folder symbol
* **Function:** Save current row configuration
* **Purpose:** Preserve all row settings
* **Scope:** Current row only

**4. Delete Icon:**

* **Icon:** Trash bin
* **Function:** Remove row from page
* **Warning:** Confirmation prompt (potentially)
* **Result:**

  * Row deleted permanently
  * Content removed
  * Cannot undo (usually)
* **Caution:** Destructive action

\---

**RIGHT SIDE CONTROLS:**

**1. Plus Icon (Top-Right Corner):**

* **Icon:** Plus symbol (+)
* **Location:** Top-right corner of row
* **Function:** Add new row above current row
* **Quick:** One-click insertion
* **Opens:** Add Rows modal (potentially)

**2. Collapse/Move Controls:**

* **Icons:** Green arrows or chevrons
* **Location:** Right edge of row
* **Functions:**

  * Move up: Shift row higher on page
  * Move down: Shift row lower on page
  * Collapse: Minimize row in editor
* **Purpose:** Section navigation and reordering

**3. Plus Icons (Right Edge):**

* **Icons:** Multiple plus symbols
* **Location:** Right edge at intervals
* **Function:** Add adjacent sections
* **Position:** Add row above/below/between

\---

**BOTTOM CENTER CONTROL:**

**Green Resize Handle:**

* **Icon:** Green square with handle indicator
* **Location:** Bottom center of row border
* **Function:** Drag to adjust row height
* **Direction:** Vertical only
* **Interaction:**

  * Click and hold
  * Drag up: Decrease height
  * Drag down: Increase height
* **Visual:** Cursor changes to resize indicator
* **Real-time:** Height adjusts as you drag

\---

**COLUMN DIVIDERS:**

**Green Vertical Lines:**

* **Location:** Between columns within row
* **Visual:** Solid green vertical lines
* **Function:** Resize column widths

**Resize Interaction:**

* Hover over divider
* Cursor changes to resize (⟷)
* Click and drag horizontally
* Left: Shrink left column, expand right
* Right: Expand left column, shrink right
* Real-time adjustment
* Proportional or independent sizing

**Width Constraints:**

* Total row width: Always 100%
* Minimum column width: Prevents collapse
* Snap points: May snap to percentage widths
* Visual feedback: Shows width during drag

\---

#### **ROW SETTINGS PANEL - STYLE TAB**

**Panel Location:** Right sidebar **Tab:** Style (brush/paint icon)

\---

**APPLY STYLE TO:**

**Section Title:** Apply Style To

**4 Device Options:** (Radio buttons or toggle group)

**1. All Devices (Default):**

* **Icon:** Multiple devices (desktop, tablet, mobile)
* **Function:** Settings apply to all screen sizes
* **Default:** Selected by default
* **Use:** Universal styling

**2. Desktop Only:**

* **Icon:** Desktop monitor
* **Function:** Settings apply only to desktop view
* **Breakpoint:** Typically 1024px and above
* **Use:** Desktop-specific adjustments

**3. Tablet Only:**

* **Icon:** Tablet device
* **Function:** Settings apply only to tablet view
* **Breakpoint:** Typically 768px to 1023px
* **Use:** Tablet-specific adjustments

**4. Mobile Only:**

* **Icon:** Mobile phone
* **Function:** Settings apply only to mobile view
* **Breakpoint:** Typically below 768px
* **Use:** Mobile-specific adjustments

**Purpose:**

* Responsive design control
* Device-specific styling
* Different layouts per breakpoint
* Optimize for each screen size

\---

**SPACING SECTION:**

**Section Header:** Spacing **Collapsible:** Yes (chevron to expand/collapse)

**Purpose:**

* External space around row (margin)
* Creates gaps between rows
* Pushes adjacent content
* Doesn't affect row background

\---

**Spacing Top:**

* **Label:** Spacing Top
* **Control:** Horizontal slider
* **Input:** Numeric field (right side)
* **Unit:** Pixels (px)
* **Range:** Typically -200 to 200 (supports negative)
* **Default:** 0
* **Example Values:** 0, 10, 20, 50, 70

**Spacing Bottom:**

* **Label:** Spacing Bottom
* **Control:** Horizontal slider
* **Input:** Numeric field
* **Unit:** Pixels (px)
* **Range:** -200 to 200
* **Default:** 0
* **Example Values:** 0, 15, 20, 50

**Spacing Left:**

* **Label:** Spacing Left
* **Control:** Horizontal slider
* **Input:** Numeric field
* **Unit:** Pixels (px)
* **Range:** -200 to 200
* **Default:** 0

**Spacing Right:**

* **Label:** Spacing Right
* **Control:** Horizontal slider
* **Input:** Numeric field
* **Unit:** Pixels (px)
* **Range:** -200 to 200
* **Default:** 0

**Slider Interaction:**

* Click and drag handle
* Real-time preview
* Blue track shows value
* Numeric input: Type exact value
* Synchronized: Slider and input linked

**Negative Values:**

* Allowed for spacing (margin)
* Creates overlapping effects
* Pull rows closer together
* Useful for full-bleed designs

\---

**PADDING SECTION:**

**Section Header:** Padding **Collapsible:** Yes (chevron to expand/collapse)

**Purpose:**

* Internal space within row
* Creates gap between row edge and content
* Background extends into padding
* Affects clickable area

\---

**Padding Top:**

* **Label:** Padding Top
* **Control:** Horizontal slider
* **Input:** Numeric field
* **Unit:** Pixels (px)
* **Range:** 0 to 200 (positive only)
* **Default:** 0

**Padding Bottom:**

* **Label:** Padding Bottom
* **Control:** Horizontal slider
* **Input:** Numeric field
* **Unit:** Pixels (px)
* **Range:** 0 to 200
* **Default:** 0

**Padding Left:**

* **Label:** Padding Left
* **Control:** Horizontal slider
* **Input:** Numeric field
* **Unit:** Pixels (px)
* **Range:** 0 to 200
* **Default:** 0
* **Example Values:** 10

**Padding Right:**

* **Label:** Padding Right
* **Control:** Horizontal slider
* **Input:** Numeric field
* **Unit:** Pixels (px)
* **Range:** 0 to 200
* **Default:** 0
* **Example Values:** 10

\---

**BORDER SETTINGS:**

**Border Corner:**

* **Label:** Border Corner
* **Input:** Numeric field
* **Unit:** Pixels (px)
* **Function:** Border radius (rounded corners)
* **Range:** 0 to 100+
* **Values:**

  * 0: Sharp corners
  * 6: Subtle rounding
  * 12-20: Noticeable rounding
  * 50+: Very rounded (can create circles)

**Border Style:**

* **Label:** Border Style
* **Control:** Dropdown selector
* **Options:**

  * **none** (default)
  * **dotted**
  * **dashed**
  * **solid**
  * **double** (potentially)
* **Function:** Border line style
* **Requirement:** Must select style other than "none" for border to show

**Border Width:**

* **Label:** Border Width
* **Input:** Numeric field
* **Unit:** Pixels (px)
* **Range:** 0 to 50+
* **Default:** 0
* **Function:** Border thickness
* **Requirement:** Must be > 0 for border to show

**Border Color:**

* **Label:** Border Color
* **Control:** Colour picker dropdown
* **Display:** Colour swatch
* **Function:** Border colour
* **Format:** Hex, RGB, RGBA
* **Requirement:** Must be set for border to show

**Border Visibility Requirements:**

* Border Style ≠ "none"
* Border Width > 0
* Border Color selected
* All three required for visible border

\---

**ALIGNMENT SETTINGS:**

**Horizontal Alignment:**

* **Label:** Horizontal Alignment
* **Control:** Dropdown selector
* **Options:**

  * **Left** - Content aligned to left
  * **Right** - Content aligned to right
  * **Center** - Content centered
  * **Justify** - Content justified (full width)
* **Function:** Horizontal positioning of content within row
* **Default:** Left (typically)

**Vertical Alignment:**

* **Label:** Vertical Alignment
* **Control:** Dropdown selector
* **Options:**

  * **Top** - Content aligned to top
  * **Center** - Content vertically centered
  * **Bottom** - Content aligned to bottom
* **Function:** Vertical positioning of content within row
* **Use:** Multi-column rows with varying content heights
* **Default:** Top (typically)

\---

**BACKGROUND SETTINGS:**

**Background Color:**

* **Label:** Background Color
* **Control:** Colour picker dropdown
* **Display:** Colour swatch
* **Features:**

  * Preset colour swatches
  * Custom colour selector
  * Gradient selector
  * Colour spectrum picker
  * Hex input field
  * RGB/RGBA values
* **Transparency:** Supported (checkered pattern indicates transparent)
* **Default:** Transparent
* **Format:** RGBA (e.g., "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)")

**Background Image:**

* **Label:** Background Image
* **Control:** Text input + upload icon
* **Display:** "none" (when no image)
* **Function:** Upload background image for row
* **Interaction:**

  * Click upload icon
  * Select image file
  * Image uploads and displays
* **File Types:** JPG, PNG, GIF, WebP
* **Position:** Controlled by Bg image position setting

**Bg image position:**

* **Label:** Bg image position
* **Control:** Dropdown selector
* **Options:**

  * **Top left** (default)
  * **Top center**
  * **Top right**
  * **Bottom left**
  * **Bottom center**
  * **Bottom right**
  * **Center center**
  * **Center left** (potentially)
  * **Center right** (potentially)
* **Function:** Position background image within row
* **Use:** Control focal point of background image

\---

**BACKGROUND OVERLAY:**

**Background Overlay Toggle:**

* **Label:** Background Overlay
* **Control:** Toggle switch (ON/OFF)
* **Default:** OFF
* **Function:** Add colour overlay on top of background image

**When Toggle is OFF:**

* Overlay controls hidden
* No overlay applied
* Background image shows clearly

**When Toggle is ON:** Reveals two additional controls:

**Overlay color:**

* **Label:** Overlay color
* **Control:** Colour picker dropdown
* **Function:** Choose overlay colour
* **Purpose:**

  * Darken/lighten background
  * Improve text readability
  * Add colour tint
  * Create mood/atmosphere
* **Common:** Dark overlay (black/dark grey) for better text contrast

**Overlay opacity:**

* **Label:** Overlay opacity
* **Control:** Horizontal slider
* **Input:** Numeric field
* **Range:** 0 to 1 (decimal)
* **Default:** 0.5
* **Values:**

  * 0: Completely transparent (no overlay)
  * 0.5: 50% opacity (moderate overlay)
  * 1: Completely opaque (blocks background)
* **Purpose:** Control overlay transparency
* **Use:** Balance between background visibility and text readability

\---

**LAYOUT OPTIONS:**

**Full Width Toggle:**

* **Label:** Full Width
* **Control:** Toggle switch (ON/OFF)
* **Default:** OFF (typically)

**When ON:**

* Section extends edge-to-edge
* Spans full browser width
* No side margins
* Background reaches edges
* Modern, immersive look

**When OFF:**

* Section respects site max-width
* Contained width (typically 1200-1400px)
* Side margins visible on large screens
* Boxed/traditional layout
* Content aligned with other sections

**Apply Branding Toggle:**

* **Label:** Apply Branding
* **Control:** Toggle switch (ON/OFF)
* **Default:** OFF (typically)

**When ON:**

* Applies site branding colours automatically
* Uses colours from SITE → Branding
* Consistent with site theme
* Overrides custom colours in this section

**When OFF:**

* Uses custom colours set in this panel
* Independent from site branding
* Custom styling for specific section
* Full control over appearance

\---

**RESET BLOCK BUTTON:**

**Button Style:**

* Blue button
* Text: "Reset Block"
* Icon: Circular arrow (potentially)

**Location:**

* Bottom of Style tab
* Below all settings

**Function:**

* Restore default row settings
* Resets ALL style settings:

  * Spacing values
  * Padding values
  * Borders
  * Alignment
  * Background
  * Overlay
  * Layout options

**Warning:**

* May show confirmation prompt
* Destructive action
* Cannot undo (usually)

**Scope:**

* Current row only
* Doesn't affect other rows
* Doesn't affect blocks within row

\---

#### **ROW SETTINGS PANEL - SETTINGS TAB**

**Panel Location:** Right sidebar **Tab:** Settings (gear/cog icon)

\---

**ANIMATION SETTINGS:**

**Animation:**

* **Label:** Animation
* **Control:** Dropdown selector
* **Default:** Normal (no animation)

**Animation Options (34 Total):**

1. **Normal** - No animation (default)
2. **Bounce** - Bouncing effect
3. **Bounce In** - Bounce in from scale
4. **Bounce Up In** - Bounce in from bottom
5. **Bounce Down In** - Bounce in from top
6. **Bounce Left In** - Bounce in from left
7. **Bounce Right In** - Bounce in from right
8. **Expand In** - Expand from center
9. **Flash** - Quick flash effect
10. **Fade In** - Fade in gradually
11. **FlipX In** - Flip horizontally
12. **FlipY In** - Flip vertically
13. **Flip BounceX In** - Flip and bounce horizontal
14. **Flip BounceY In** - Flip and bounce vertical
15. **Perspective Up In** - 3D perspective from bottom
16. **Perspective Down In** - 3D perspective from top
17. **Perspective Left In** - 3D perspective from left
18. **Perspective Right In** - 3D perspective from right
19. **Pulse** - Pulsing scale effect
20. **Shake** - Shaking motion
21. **Shrink In** - Shrink in from large
22. **Slide Up In** - Slide in from bottom
23. **Slide Down In** - Slide in from top
24. **Slide Left In** - Slide in from right
25. **Slide Right In** - Slide in from left
26. **Slide Up Big In** - Large slide from bottom
27. **Slide Down Big In** - Large slide from top
28. **Slide Left Big In** - Large slide from right
29. **Slide Right Big In** - Large slide from left
30. **Swing** - Swinging motion
31. **Swoop In** - Swooping entrance
32. **Tada** - Tada celebration effect
33. **Whirl In** - Whirling entrance

**Function:**

* Entrance animation when row comes into view
* Scroll-triggered (typically)
* Adds visual interest
* Draws attention to content

\---

**Delay (seconds):**

* **Label:** Delay (seconds)
* **Control:** Numeric input field
* **Icon:** Info icon (tooltip with details)
* **Default:** 0
* **Range:** 0 to 10+ seconds
* **Function:** Controls animation start delay
* **Use:**

  * Sequence multiple animations
  * Stagger entrance timing
  * Create choreographed effects
* **Value:** Time in seconds before animation starts

**Interval (seconds):**

* **Label:** Interval (seconds)
* **Control:** Numeric input field
* **Icon:** Info icon (tooltip with details)
* **Default:** 0
* **Range:** 0 to 60+ seconds
* **Function:** Controls animation repeat interval
* **Values:**

  * 0: Play animation once only
  * 0: Repeat animation at specified interval
* **Use:**

  * Continuous animations
  * Periodic attention-grabbing
  * Looping effects

\---

**RESPONSIVE VISIBILITY:**

**Hide on:**

* **Label:** Hide on
* **Control:** Multi-select dropdown
* **Placeholder:** "Select Devices"
* **"select all" Link:** At top of dropdown (check all options)

**Checkboxes:**

* **Desktop** - Hide on desktop (1024px+)
* **Tablet** - Hide on tablet (768px-1023px)
* **Mobile** - Hide on mobile (<768px)

**Function:**

* Responsive visibility control
* Show/hide row per device type
* Optimize layouts for different screens

**Use Cases:**

* Hide large images on mobile
* Show mobile-specific CTAs
* Desktop-only complex layouts
* Mobile-only simplified content

**Examples:**

* Check "Mobile": Row hidden on mobile, visible on tablet/desktop
* Check "Desktop, Tablet": Row hidden except on mobile
* Check "Desktop, Mobile": Row visible only on tablet
* Check none: Row visible on all devices

\---

**RESET BLOCK BUTTON:**

**Button Style:**

* Blue button
* Text: "Reset Block"
* Icon: Circular arrow (potentially)

**Location:**

* Bottom of Settings tab

**Function:**

* Restore default settings values
* Resets:

  * Animation to Normal
  * Delay to 0
  * Interval to 0
  * Hide on to none (visible all devices)

**Warning:**

* May show confirmation prompt
* Destructive action

**Column-Level Controls:**

**Control Location:** Top of selected column

**Icons:**

**Lock/Template Icon:**

* **Location:** Left side
* **Function:** Save column as template
* **Purpose:**

  * Save column configuration
  * Reuse column layout
  * Preserve content and styling

**Column Resize:**

**Green Vertical Dividers:**

* Location between columns
* Drag handle on divider line

**Interaction:**

* Hover: Cursor changes to resize (⟷)
* Click and drag horizontally
* Real-time width adjustment
* Visual feedback during drag

**Behavior:**

* **Proportional:** Other columns adjust automatically
* **Independent:** Can set specific widths
* **Constraints:** Total row width = 100%
* **Minimum:** Columns have minimum width
* **Snap:** May snap to percentage widths (25%, 33%, 50%, etc.)
