**Nested Selection Hierarchy:**

**Level 1: Row/Section**

* **Indicator:** Green border and controls
* **Scope:** Entire row with all columns
* **Settings:** Row-wide styling and layout

**Level 2: Column**

* **Indicator:** Red border around column, dotted grey around row
* **Scope:** Individual column within row
* **Settings:** Column-specific width and content

**Level 3: Content Block**

* **Indicator:** Red border around block
* **Scope:** Individual block within column
* **Settings:** Block-specific content and styling

**Purpose:**

* Clear editing context
* Visual hierarchy
* Prevents confusion
* Shows relationships

**Spacing Bottom:**

**TYPOGRAPHY:**

**Font:**

* **Label:** Font
* **Control:** Dropdown selector
* **Sources:**

  * System fonts (Arial, Times New Roman, etc.)
  * Custom uploaded fonts
  * Google Fonts library (900+ fonts)
* **Examples:**

  * Montserrat
  * Lato
  * Open Sans
  * Roboto
  * Patrick Hand
  * Growtesk
* **Searchable:** Filter font list by name

**Font Style:**

* **Label:** Font Style
* **Control:** Dropdown selector
* **Availability:** Depends on selected font family

**Options:**

* **Thin** - Ultra-light weight
* **Thin Italic** - Ultra-light slanted
* **Light** - Light weight
* **Light Italic** - Light slanted
* **Regular** - Normal weight (default)
* **Regular Italic** - Normal slanted
* **Medium** - Medium weight
* **Medium Italic** - Medium slanted
* **Bold** - Heavy weight
* **Bold Italic** - Heavy slanted
* **Black** - Extra bold weight
* **Black Italic** - Extra bold slanted

**Note:** Not all fonts have all styles

**Font Size:**

* **Label:** Font Size
* **Control:** Numeric input
* **Unit:** Pixels (px)
* **Example Values:**

  * 16px (body text)
  * 22.4px (medium heading)
  * 30px (large heading)
* **Range:** Typically 10-100px

**Color:**

* **Label:** Color
* **Control:** Colour picker dropdown
* **Format:** Hex, RGB, RGBA
* **Purpose:** Text colour

**Letter Spacing:**

* **Label:** Letter Spacing
* **Control:** Numeric input
* **Unit:** Pixels (px)
* **Values:**

  * Negative (-1 to -3px): Tight/compact, modern feel
  * Zero (0px): Normal spacing
  * Positive (1-5px): Loose/airy, elegant feel
* **Examples:**

  * \-1px (tight)
  * 0px (normal)

**Line Height:**

* **Label:** Line Height
* **Control:** Numeric input
* **Unit:** Pixels (px)
* **Examples:**

  * 22.4px (body text)
  * 33.26px (medium text)
  * 42px (heading)
* **Formula:** Line height ÷ Font size = ratio
* **Guidelines:**

  * Body text: 1.4-1.6× font size
  * Headings: 1.1-1.3× font size

\---

**BACKGROUND:**

**Background Color:**

* **Label:** Background Color
* **Control:** Colour picker dropdown
* **Transparency:** Supported (checkered pattern)
* **Purpose:** Background behind text
* **Default:** Transparent

**Background Image:**

* **Label:** Background Image
* **Control:** Text input + upload icon
* **Display:** "none" (when no image)
* **Purpose:** Background image for text block

\---

**TEXT EFFECTS:**

**Text Transform:**

* **Label:** Text Transform
* **Control:** Dropdown selector
* **Options:**

  * **None** - No transformation
  * **Uppercase** - ALL CAPS
  * **Lowercase** - all lowercase
  * **Capitalize** - First Letter Of Each Word

**Text Shadow:**

* **Label:** Text Shadow
* **Control:** Dropdown selector
* **Options:**

  * **None** - No shadow
  * **Light** - Subtle text shadow
  * **Medium** - Moderate text shadow
  * **Dark** - Strong text shadow
* **Purpose:**

  * Improve readability on busy backgrounds
  * Add depth to text
  * Create visual interest

**Alignment:**

* **Label:** Alignment
* **Control:** Dropdown selector
* **Options:**

  * **Left** - Text aligned to left
  * **Center** - Text centered
  * **Right** - Text aligned to right
  * **Justify** - Text justified (full width)

#### **TEXT EDITING WORKFLOW**

**Step 1: Select Text Block**

* Click on text content
* Red border appears
* Toolbar displays at top
* Settings panel opens on right

**Step 2: Inline Editing**

* Click text to edit directly
* Cursor appears in text
* Type new content
* Standard text editing (select, cut, copy, paste)
* Changes visible in real-time

**Step 3: Apply Styling**

* Use right sidebar settings panel
* Adjust fonts, sizes, colours
* Modify spacing and padding
* Apply text effects
* Changes apply immediately
* Live preview in editor

**Step 4: AI Enhancement (Optional)**

* Click "Zenler AI" button
* AI interface opens
* Use AI features for content:

  * Generate new text
  * Improve existing text
  * Adjust tone
  * Fix grammar
  * Optimize for SEO
* Accept or refine suggestions
* AI-generated content inserted

**Step 5: Save/Deselect**

* Click X button to deselect
* Changes save automatically
* Block returns to normal view
* No manual save required

### **IMAGE BLOCK EDITING**

#### **IMAGE BLOCK TOOLBAR (RED)**

**Toolbar Location:** Top of selected image block **Toolbar Style:** Red background, white icons

\---

**LEFT SIDE CONTROLS:**

**1. Lock/save template:**

* Save image block as template
* Preserve styling and settings

**2. Duplicate:**

* Clone image block
* Create identical copy

**3. Folder/save:**

* Save image configuration
* Preserve settings

**4. Delete:**

* Trash icon
* Remove image block

\---

**RESIZE HANDLES:**

**Corner Handles:**

* All four corners (top-left, top-right, bottom-left, bottom-right)
* **Function:** Proportional resize
* **Interaction:**

  * Click and drag corner
  * Maintains aspect ratio (usually)
  * Can force distortion (Shift key potentially)

**Edge Handles:**

* Top, bottom, left, right edges
* **Function:** Independent width/height adjustment
* **Interaction:**

  * Click and drag edge
  * Adjusts one dimension
  * Can distort aspect ratio

**Visual:**

* Small squares or circles
* Red colour matching toolbar
* Visible when image selected

\---

#### **IMAGE BLOCK SETTINGS - SETTINGS TAB**

**Panel Location:** Right sidebar **Tab:** Settings (gear icon)

\---

**IMAGE CONFIGURATION:**

**Image Url:**

* **Label:** Image Url
* **Control:** Text input field
* **Function:** Direct URL input for image source
* **Format:** Complete image URL
* **Example:** "\~70mb$Sc-kj..." (truncated)
* **Sources:**

  * Uploaded images
  * External URLs
  * CDN links
* **Required:** Yes (for image to display)

**On Click Go to:**

* **Label:** On Click Go to
* **Control:** Text input field
* **Function:** Link destination when image clicked
* **Format:** URL (internal or external)
* **Example:** "[http://clothbro](http://clothbro)..."
* **Optional:** Can be left empty (no link)
* **Use Cases:**

  * Product pages
  * Landing pages
  * External websites
  * Pop-ups

**Open in new tab:**

* **Label:** Open in new tab
* **Control:** Toggle switch
* **States:**

  * OFF (grey) - Link opens in same tab
  * ON (teal/green) - Link opens in new tab/window
* **Examples Show:** OFF state
* **Best Practice:** ON for external links
* **Purpose:** Keep visitors on site

\---

**ACCESSIBILITY \& SEO:**

**Title attribute:**

* **Label:** Title attribute
* **Control:** Text input field
* **HTML Attribute:** title=""
* **Function:** Hover tooltip text
* **Shows:** When mouse hovers over image
* **Purpose:**

  * Additional context
  * Accessibility benefit
  * SEO benefit (minor)
* **Optional:** Can be left empty

**Alt attribute:**

* **Label:** Alt attribute
* **Control:** Text input field
* **HTML Attribute:** alt=""
* **Function:** Alternative text describing image
* **Shows:**

  * When image fails to load
  * Read by screen readers
  * Displayed if images disabled
* **Purpose:**

  * **Accessibility:** Required for screen readers
  * **SEO:** Helps search engines understand image
  * **User Experience:** Context when image unavailable
* **Best Practice:** Describe image content clearly
* **Required:** Yes (for accessibility compliance)
* **Examples:**

  * "Woman using laptop in coffee shop"
  * "Blue running shoes on white background"
  * "Company logo"

**ANIMATION \& VISIBILITY:**

**Animation:**

* **Control:** Dropdown selector
* **Default:** "Normal"
* **Options:** Same 33 animation options as text/row

**Delay (seconds):**

* **Control:** Numeric input with info icon
* **Default:** 0
* **Function:** Animation start delay

**Interval (seconds):**

* **Control:** Numeric input with info icon
* **Default:** 0
* **Function:** Animation repeat interval

**Hide on:**

* **Control:** Multi-select dropdown
* **Placeholder:** "Select Devices"
* **Options:** Desktop, Tablet, Mobile
* **Function:** Responsive visibility control

**BORDER SETTINGS:**

**Border Corner:**

* **Label:** Border Corner
* **Control:** Numeric input
* **Unit:** Pixels (px)
* **Example:** 6px
* **Function:** Rounded corners on image
* **Values:**

  * 0: Sharp corners
  * 6: Subtle rounding
  * 12-20: Noticeable rounding
  * 50+: Very rounded

**Border Style:**

* **Label:** Border Style
* **Control:** Dropdown selector
* **Default:** "none"
* **Options:**

  * **none** - No border
  * **solid** - Solid line
  * **dashed** - Dashed line
  * **dotted** - Dotted line
  * **double** - Double line

**Border Width:**

* **Label:** Border Width
* **Control:** Numeric input
* **Unit:** Pixels (px)
* **Default:** 0
* **Range:** 0-50+

**Border Color:**

* **Label:** Border Color
* **Control:** Colour picker dropdown
* **Example:** Light grey colour shown
* **Function:** Border outline colour
* **Works With:** Border Style and Width

**Border Requirements:**

* Style ≠ "none"
* Width > 0
* Color selected
* All three required for visible border

**IMAGE STYLING:**

**Image Style:**

* **Label:** Image Style
* **Control:** Dropdown selector
* **Example:** "Rounded" (shown)
* **Function:** Pre-defined image styling presets

**Likely Options:**

* **None/Normal** - No special styling
* **Rounded** - Rounded corners
* **Circle** - Circular image (requires square source)
* **Square** - Force square aspect
* **Thumbnail** - Thumbnail frame effect
* **Custom** - Custom styling

**Purpose:**

* Quick styling without manual border settings
* Professional presets
* Consistent image presentation

**Alignment:**

* **Label:** Alignment
* **Control:** Dropdown selector
* **Default:** "Left"
* **Options:**

  * **Left** - Image aligned to left of container
  * **Center** - Image centered in container
  * **Right** - Image aligned to right of container
* **Function:** Controls image position within container
* **Container:** Parent column or block

### **VIDEO BLOCK EDITING**

#### **VIDEO BLOCK DISPLAY**

**Normal State (No Video URL):**

**Visual Appearance:**

* Black background rectangle
* Error message displayed
* Icon: Circle with exclamation mark
* Text: "Video unavailable"
* Subtext: "This video is unavailable"
* YouTube logo visible (bottom right)
* Aspect ratio: 16:9 (standard video)

**Error State Appears When:**

* No URL entered in settings
* Invalid URL provided
* Video removed or deleted
* Video set to private
* Connectivity issues prevent loading
* Embed restrictions from video host

**Purpose:**

* Placeholder for video embed
* Visual indicator of missing/error video
* Maintains layout structure

* Top, bottom, left, right
* Independent dimension adjustment
* Can adjust aspect ratio

**VIDEO CONFIGURATION:**

**Video Url:**

* **Label:** Video Url
* **Control:** Text input field
* **Example:** "[https://www](https://www)..." (truncated)
* **Required:** Yes (for video to display)

**Supported Video Sources:**

**1. YouTube:**

* Standard URLs: [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=\[ID](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=%5BID)]
* Short URLs: [https://youtu.be/\[ID](https://youtu.be/%5BID)]
* Embedded player with YouTube branding
* Standard YouTube player controls

**2. Vimeo:**

* Standard URLs: [https://vimeo.com/\[ID](https://vimeo.com/%5BID)]
* Embedded Vimeo player
* Vimeo player controls
* Privacy settings respected

**3. Direct Video URLs:**

* MP4 format: video.mp4
* WebM format: video.webm
* Other HTML5 video formats
* HTML5 video player
* Browser-native controls

**Format Examples:**

* [https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dQw4w9WgXcQ](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dQw4w9WgXcQ)
* [https://vimeo.com/123456789](https://vimeo.com/123456789)
* [https://example.com/videos/sample.mp4](https://example.com/videos/sample.mp4)

**Delay (seconds):**

* **Label:** Delay (seconds)
* **Control:** Numeric input with info icon
* **Default:** 0
* **Function:** Controls animation start delay
* **Range:** 0-10+ seconds

**Interval (seconds):**

* **Label:** Interval (seconds)
* **Control:** Numeric input with info icon
* **Default:** 0
* **Function:** Controls animation repeat timing
* **Values:**

  * 0: Play animation once only
  * 0: Repeat at specified interval

**Hide on:**

* **Placeholder:** "Select Devices"

  * Desktop checkbox
  * Tablet checkbox

* **Function:** Responsive visibility control

#### **SAVE AS TEMPLATE FEATURE**

**Lock Icon Location:**

* Available on: Row, Column, and Block toolbars
* **Icon:** Lock/padlock symbol
* **Tooltip:** "Save Block As Template" (on hover)

**Functionality:**

**For Rows:**

* Saves entire row layout structure
* Preserves all columns
* Includes all content within row
* Saves all row settings:

  * Spacing
  * Padding
  * Borders
  * Background
  * Alignment
  * Animations
* Reusable across pages

**For Columns:**

* Saves column configuration
* Preserves column content
* Saves column width (potentially)
* Maintains internal structure

**For Blocks:**

* Saves individual block
* Preserves content
* Saves all styling:

  * Fonts
  * Colors
  * Spacing
  * Effects
* Block-specific settings

**Template Library:**

* Saved templates available in library
* Accessible from Add Rows/Blocks modal
* Reusable across site
* Time-saving feature

**Use Cases:**

* Consistent section designs
* Reusable content blocks
* Standard layouts
* Brand consistency
* Rapid page building

\---

#### **RESIZE FUNCTIONALITY**

**Row Height:**

**Handle Location:** Bottom center of row (green square)

**Interaction:**

* Click and hold handle
* Drag vertically:

  * Up: Decrease row height
  * Down: Increase row height
* Visual feedback: Cursor changes to ↕
* Real-time preview

**Behavior:**

* Content reflows as needed
* Minimum height enforced
* Maximum height: Unlimited
* Maintains layout structure

\---

**Column Width:**

**Handle Location:** Green vertical dividers between columns

**Interaction:**

* Hover over divider
* Cursor changes to ⟷ (horizontal resize)
* Click and drag horizontally:

  * Left: Shrink left column, expand right column
  * Right: Expand left column, shrink right column

**Behavior:**

* Real-time adjustment
* Visual feedback during drag
* Columns maintain proportions or adjust independently
* Total row width always 100%
* Minimum column width enforced
* Snap to percentage widths (potentially)

**Visual Feedback:**

* Column widths adjust as you drag
* Content reflows dynamically
* Width percentage may display
* Smooth animation

\---

**Block Dimensions:**

**Handles Location:**

* Corner handles (all four corners)
* Edge handles (top, bottom, left, right)
* Red colour for blocks

**Interaction:**

* Click and drag handles
* Corner: Proportional resize (usually)
* Edge: Independent dimension

**Behavior:**

* Can maintain aspect ratio
* Can force distortion
* Content scales or reflows
* Minimum size enforced
* Real-time preview

**Aspect Ratio:**

* Images: Often maintain ratio
* Video: Maintain 16:9 (typically)
* Text: Height adjusts, width from container

\---

#### **SETTINGS PANEL BEHAVIOR**

**Fixed Position:**

* Right sidebar location
* Fixed while scrolling main editor
* Always accessible during editing
* Consistent position

**Scrollable Content:**

* Panel scrolls independently
* Multiple collapsible sections
* Smooth scroll through settings
* Always visible controls

**Collapsible Sections:**

**Chevron Icons:**

* **Point Down:** Section expanded (content visible)
* **Point Right:** Section collapsed (content hidden)
* **Interaction:** Click header to toggle

**Sections:**

* Spacing (collapsible)
* Padding (collapsible)
* Font settings (open by default)
* Border settings (open by default)
* Background settings (open by default)

**Multiple Sections Open:**

* Can expand multiple sections simultaneously
* Easy comparison of related settings
* Quick access to frequently used controls
* Scroll to view all expanded sections

**Smooth Scrolling:**

* Panel scrolls smoothly
* Scroll bar on right edge
* Mouse wheel support
* Touch-friendly on tablets

\---

**Device-Specific Settings:**

**Apply Style To Selector:**

* Location: Top of Style tab
* Four options: All Devices, Desktop Only, Tablet Only, Mobile Only

**Function:**

* When specific device selected:

  * Settings apply only to that device
  * Different values per breakpoint
  * Allows responsive customization
* When "All Devices" selected:

  * Settings apply universally
  * Single value for all screens

**Purpose:**

* Responsive design control
* Device-specific optimization
* Different spacing on mobile vs desktop
* Optimized typography per screen size

**Example Use:**

* Desktop: Large font size (30px)
* Tablet: Medium font size (24px)
* Mobile: Small font size (18px)

  * Vimeo: [https://vimeo.com/\[ID](https://vimeo.com/%5BID)]

* Video loads and displays automatically
* Error message disappears

**Select Row:**

* Click between columns or on row edge
* Green selection border appears
* Row toolbar displays

**Resize Columns:**

* Drag green vertical divider between columns
* Adjust left/right to change column widths

**Adjust Spacing:**

* Open settings panel (right sidebar)
* Style tab active

  * Spacing (margin around row)

**Step 5: Customize Styling**

**Apply Animations:**

* Select row or blocks
* Settings tab in panel
* Choose animation from dropdown

**Responsive Visibility:**

* Use "Hide on" options
* Show/hide per device type

**Colors and Fonts:**

* Adjust text colors

**Preview:**

* Click device selector (top toolbar)

**Step 6: Save and Publish**

**Save Page:**

* Click "Save" button in top toolbar
* All changes saved automatically
* Continue editing or preview

**Preview Page:**

* Click "Preview" button
* Opens in new tab

**Publish:**

* Return to Pages menu (or use toolbar)

* Page goes live when published
* Accessible to users based on privacy settings

**Final Checks:**

* Video plays correctly
* Text is error-free
* Layout looks professional
* Responsive on all devices
* Links work (if any)

## **SPACING AND LAYOUT CONVENTIONS**

### **Spacing vs Padding**

**Spacing (Margin):**

* **Definition:** External space around element
* **Function:** Creates gaps between elements
* **Behavior:** Pushes adjacent content away
* **Background:** Doesn't affect background colour/image
* **Values:** Can be negative for overlapping effects
* **Use Cases:**

  * Gaps between rows
  * Separation between sections
  * Space above/below content
  * Pull elements closer (negative values)

**Padding:**

* **Definition:** Internal space within element
* **Function:** Creates gap between element edge and content
* **Behavior:** Background extends into padding area
* **Values:** Always positive
* **Effect:** Affects clickable area (larger padding = larger click target)
* **Use Cases:**

  * Space around text within block
  * Button internal spacing
  * Content breathing room
  * Background color extension

\---

**Visual Difference:**

```
┌─────────────────────┐  ← Spacing (margin) - external
│                     │
│  ┌───────────────┐  │  ← Padding - internal
│  │   Content     │  │  ← Content area
│  └───────────────┘  │
│                     │
└─────────────────────┘
```

\---

**Default Units:**

* All spacing and padding uses pixels (px) throughout
* Consistent measurement system
* Precise control
* Industry standard

**Negative Spacing:**

* **Allowed:** Yes, for spacing (margin) only
* **Effect:** Creates overlapping effects
* **Use Cases:**

  * Full-bleed designs
  * Overlapping sections
  * Pull elements closer together
  * Creative layouts
* **Example:** Spacing Top: -50px (pulls section up by 50px)

**Padding (Never Negative):**

* **Allowed:** No, padding cannot be negative
* **Values:** 0 to unlimited positive numbers
* **Minimum:** 0 (no padding)

\---

**Responsive Considerations:**

* **Device-specific values supported**
* Different spacing/padding per breakpoint
* Use "Apply Style To" selector in Style tab
* **Example:**

  * Desktop: Spacing Bottom 50px
  * Tablet: Spacing Bottom 30px
  * Mobile: Spacing Bottom 20px
* Optimizes spacing for each screen size

\---

### **Typography Guidance**

**Recommended Sizes:**

**Headings:**

* **Large Hero Headings:** 36-48px

  * Main page headline
  * Landing page titles
  * Maximum impact
* **Section Headings:** 24-36px

  * H1, H2 level
  * Section titles
  * Feature headings
* **Subheadings:** 18-24px

  * H3, H4 level
  * Subsection titles
  * Card headings

**Body Text:**

* **Standard Body:** 14-18px

  * Paragraphs
  * Descriptions
  * Main content
  * Most readable
* **Large Body:** 18-22px

  * Introductions
  * Lead paragraphs
  * Emphasis text
* **Small Text:** 12-14px

  * Captions
  * Footnotes
  * Metadata
  * Legal text

**Mobile Considerations:**

* **General Rule:** 10-20% smaller than desktop
* **Minimum:** Never below 14px for body text
* **Maintain Readability:** Contrast, line height
* **Test:** On actual devices
* **Touch Targets:** Larger tap areas (buttons, links)

\---

**Letter Spacing:**

**Negative (-1 to -3px):**

* **Effect:** Tight/compact spacing
* **Feel:** Modern, sleek
* **Best For:** Headlines, logos, modern designs
* **Example:** -1px for "NEW Zenler platform" heading

**Zero (0px):**

* **Effect:** Normal, standard spacing
* **Feel:** Default, neutral
* **Best For:** Most body text, default state
* **Example:** 0px for most readable content

**Positive (1-5px):**

* **Effect:** Loose/airy spacing
* **Feel:** Elegant, luxurious, spacious
* **Best For:** Luxury brands, elegant designs, all-caps text
* **Example:** 3px for luxury brand headlines

**Guidelines:**

* Use negative for tight, modern feel
* Use zero for standard readability
* Use positive for luxury, emphasis, all-caps
* Don't exceed -3px or +5px typically
* Test readability

\---

**Line Height:**

**Body Text Guidelines:**

* **Ratio:** 1.4-1.6× font size
* **Example:** 16px font = 22.4-25.6px line height
* **Purpose:** Good readability for paragraph text
* **Effect:** Comfortable reading, not cramped

**Headings Guidelines:**

* **Ratio:** 1.1-1.3× font size
* **Example:** 30px font = 33-39px line height
* **Purpose:** Tighter for more impact
* **Effect:** Strong visual presence, less space

**Formula:**

* Line Height ÷ Font Size = Ratio
* Example: 42px line height ÷ 30px font = 1.4 ratio

**Best Practices:**

* Larger fonts (headings): Tighter line height (1.1-1.3)
* Smaller fonts (body): Looser line height (1.4-1.6)
* Long-form reading: More generous (1.5-1.8)
* Short text (buttons, labels): Tighter (1.0-1.2)
* Test readability with actual content

\---

### **Border and Styling**

**Border Requirements:**

For a border to be visible, ALL three must be set:

1. **Border Style:** Must not be "none"
2. **Border Width:** Must be greater than 0
3. **Border Color:** Must be selected

**Missing any = no visible border**

\---

**Border Style Options:**

* **none:** No border (default)
* **solid:** Continuous solid line
* **dashed:** Dashed line ----
* **dotted:** Dotted line ····
* **double:** Double line (requires width ≥3px)

\---

**Border Width:**

* **0px:** No border (invisible)
* **1-2px:** Subtle, thin border
* **3-5px:** Medium, noticeable border
* **6-10px:** Thick, prominent border
* **>10px:** Very thick, design element

\---

**Border Radius (Corner):**

**Values \& Effects:**

* **0px:** Sharp, 90-degree corners
* **4-8px:** Subtle rounding, modern
* **12-20px:** Noticeable rounding, friendly
* **50%+:** Very rounded, can create circles

**Circle Creation:**

* Requires square element (equal width/height)
* Border radius: 50% or higher
* Creates perfect circle
* Common for avatars, icons

**Example Values:**

* 0: Sharp corners (traditional)
* 6: Subtle (modern cards)
* 12: Noticeable (buttons)
* 20: Rounded (pills, tags)
* 50%: Circle (avatars)

\---

**Image Styling Presets:**

**Purpose:**

* Pre-defined image styling options
* Quick application without manual settings
* Professional presets
* Consistent presentation

**Options (Likely):**

* **None/Normal:** No special styling, default
* **Rounded:** Border radius applied, modern cards
* **Circle:** Circular crop (requires square image)
* **Square:** Force square aspect ratio
* **Thumbnail:** Thumbnail frame effect, border
* **Custom:** Apply custom border/radius settings

**Benefits:**

* Quick styling
* Consistent appearance
* No manual border configuration needed
* Professional results

\---

## **FIELD PATTERNS AND CONTROLS**

### **Sliders**

**Appearance:**

* Horizontal bar (track)
* Blue colored portion (indicates value)
* Draggable handle (thumb)
* Numeric input field on right side
* Unit label (px) after input

**Functionality:**

**Drag Interaction:**

* Click and hold handle
* Drag left: Decrease value
* Drag right: Increase value
* Real-time visual preview in editor
* Smooth adjustment

**Numeric Input:**

* Type exact value
* Immediate update
* Validates input
* Updates slider position
* Synchronized with slider

**Range:**

* Minimum: Typically 0 or -200
* Maximum: Typically 200 or more
* Depends on property

**Visual Feedback:**

* Blue track shows current value
* Handle position indicates setting
* Changes reflect immediately in editor
* No need to click "apply"

**Use Cases:**

* Spacing adjustments
* Padding control
* Opacity settings
* Any incremental value

\---

### **Colour Pickers**

**Activation:**

* Click colour field/swatch
* Colour picker modal opens
* Large interface for colour selection

**Interface Components:**

**Preset Swatches:**

* Grid of common colours
* Quick selection
* Click to apply
* Brand colours (potentially)
* Recently used colours

**Custom Colour Selector:**

* Large colour spectrum square
* Click to choose hue/saturation
* Drag for precise selection
* Real-time preview

**Gradient Slider:**

* Vertical slider for brightness/value
* Drag to adjust lightness
* Combined with spectrum for full control

**Hex Code Input:**

* Text field for hex values
* Format: #RRGGBB
* Example: `#5134e7`
* Type or paste hex codes
* Validates format

**RGBA Value Display:**

* Red, Green, Blue, Alpha values
* Separate inputs (potentially)
* Example: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)
* Precise colour control

**Transparency Option:**

* Checkered pattern indicates transparency
* Alpha slider or input
* Range: 0 (transparent) to 1 (opaque)
* Useful for overlays, backgrounds

**RGB/HSL Modes:**

* Switch between colour modes
* RGB: Red, Green, Blue
* HSL: Hue, Saturation, Lightness
* Different selection methods

**Close Button:**

* X in corner
* Click to close picker
* Saves selection automatically

\---

**Visual Indicators:**

* Current colour swatch shown
* Checkered pattern for transparency
* Selected colour preview
* Before/after comparison (potentially)

**Use Cases:**

* Text colours
* Background colours
* Border colours
* Overlay colours
* Button colours

\---

### **Toggle Switches**

**Visual States:**

**ON State:**

* **Color:** Teal/green
* **Position:** Handle on right
* **Indicates:** Feature enabled
* **Example:** Full Width ON

**OFF State:**

* **Color:** Grey
* **Position:** Handle on left
* **Indicates:** Feature disabled
* **Example:** Full Width OFF

**Interaction:**

* Click anywhere on switch
* Toggles between ON/OFF
* Immediate state change
* Visual feedback instant

\---

**Usage:**

* Binary options (yes/no, enable/disable)
* Quick feature toggling
* Visual state indication
* Instant feedback
* Clear current state

**Examples:**

* Full Width: ON/OFF
* Apply Branding: ON/OFF
* Open in new tab: ON/OFF
* Background Overlay: ON/OFF
* Hide Community Menu: ON/OFF

**Benefits:**

* Clear visual state
* Easy to understand
* Quick to toggle
* Space-efficient
* Intuitive interaction

\---

### **Numeric Inputs**

**Appearance:**

* Text input field
* Unit label (px, %, em, etc.)
* Sometimes with up/down arrows
* Clear border/outline

**Features:**

**Direct Entry:**

* Click field
* Type number
* Press Enter or Tab
* Value updates immediately

**Validation:**

* Minimum value enforced
* Maximum value enforced (sometimes)
* Decimal support (for some fields)
* Integer only (for others)

**Negative Value Support:**

* **Spacing (margin):** Yes, supports negative
* **Font size:** No, positive only
* **Border width:** No, positive only
* **Opacity:** 0-1 decimal range only

**Unit Selectors:**

* Some fields have unit dropdown
* Options: px, %, em, rem
* Depends on property type
* Most common: px (pixels)

\---

**Examples:**

**Font Size:**

* Positive integers only
* Range: Typically 10-100px
* No decimal (usually)
* Example: 30

**Spacing:**

* Supports negative values
* Range: -200 to 200px
* Allows overlap effects
* Example: -50

**Border Width:**

* Positive only
* Range: 0-50+px
* Integer values
* Example: 2

**Opacity:**

* Decimal range
* Values: 0 to 1
* Example: 0.5
* 0 = transparent, 1 = opaque

\---

### **Dropdowns**

**Appearance:**

* Current value displayed
* Dropdown arrow indicator (▼)
* Click to expand
* Border/background

**Functionality:**

**Interaction:**

* Click field or arrow
* Dropdown menu opens
* List of options shown
* Click option to select
* Menu closes automatically

**Selection:**

* Single selection only
* Current value shown in field
* Checkmark next to selected (potentially)
* Updates immediately

**Scrolling:**

* Long lists are scrollable
* Mouse wheel supported
* Scroll bar shown if needed

**Search/Filter:**

* Some dropdowns have search
* Type to filter options
* Real-time filtering
* Examples: Font dropdown

**Visual Hierarchy:**

* Clear option labels
* Grouped options (some dropdowns)
* Separators between groups
* Consistent styling

\---

**Types:**

**Simple Lists:**

* Flat list of options
* Examples: Alignment (Left, Right, Center, Justify)
* Border Style (none, solid, dashed, dotted)

**Grouped Options:**

* Categories/groups
* Examples: Fonts by category
* Sub-headings for groups

**Multi-Level:**

* Nested selections
* Example: Font → Font Style
* First select font, then style

\---

**Examples:**

**Alignment:**

* Options: Left, Right, Center, Justify
* Single level
* 4 options

**Font:**

* Hundreds of options

### **Multi-Select Dropdowns**

**Appearance:**

* Dropdown field

* Dropdown arrow
* Opens to show checkboxes

**Features:**

**Checkboxes:**

* Each option has checkbox
* Click to toggle
* Multiple selections allowed
* Independent selection

**"Select All" Option:**

* Located at top of list
* Checkbox or link
* Click to select all options
* Click again to deselect all

**Current Selections Displayed:**

* Field shows selected items
* Or count: "2 selected"
* Clear indication of selections

**Interaction:**

* Click field to open
* Click checkboxes to select/deselect
* Multiple clicks allowed
* Click outside to close (potentially)
* Or close button

\---

**Example: "Hide on" Device Selector**

**Options:**

* ☐ Desktop
* ☐ Tablet
* ☐ Mobile

**Use Cases:**

* Hide on Desktop + Mobile (show on Tablet only)
* Hide on Mobile (show on Desktop + Tablet)
* Hide on Desktop (show on Tablet + Mobile)
* Check none: Visible on all devices

**Flexible Control:**

* Any combination possible
* Responsive visibility
* Device-specific optimization
* Complex rules supported

### **12. PRICING SECTION - COMPLETE DOCUMENTATION**

### **PRICING SECTION OVERVIEW**

**Navigation Path:** Site / Language / Pricing

**Section Purpose:** Customise pricing-related text strings displayed throughout the platform for different subscription and payment plan configurations. Allows platform owners to override default pricing language for all billing scenarios.

\---

### **PRICING SECTION INTERFACE**

**Enable Custom Pricing Language:**

* **Toggle switch** (ON/OFF)
* **Information icon (i)** with tooltip
* **Tooltip text:** "If it is enabled, the below texts will be displayed whenever pricing appears, otherwise default language texts will be displayed"
* **"Reset All" button** (top right)
* **Reset warning:** "If you reset all texts, all the pricing texts you saved will be reset to defaults."

**Section State:**

* Collapsible section with up/down arrow
* When enabled, custom pricing text overrides default language
* When disabled, default platform language applies

## **CONFIGURATION VARIABLES EXPLAINED**

### **Billing Frequencies**

**Monthly** - Recurring charge every month  
**Yearly** - Recurring charge every year  
**Daily** - Recurring charge every day  
**Weekly** - Recurring charge every week  
**Every 2 Weeks** - Recurring charge every two weeks (bi-weekly)  
**Every Custom Months** - Recurring charge at custom monthly intervals

### **Additional One-Time Fee**

**With Additional One Time Fee** - Setup or enrollment fee added to the first payment  
**Without Additional One Time Fee** - Standard recurring charges only, no additional upfront costs

### **Trial Days (Subscription Plans Only)**

**With trial days** - Free trial period before billing starts  
**Without trial days** - Billing starts immediately upon enrollment  
**And trial days** - Includes trial period in the configuration  
**No trial days mentioned** - No trial period included

### **Coupon Application Types**

**Coupon First Billing** - Discount applies only to the first payment  
**Coupon Every Billing** - Discount applies to every recurring payment throughout the subscription  
**Coupon Custom** - Custom discount rules with flexible application logic

\---

## **KEY DIFFERENCES: SUBSCRIPTION VS PAYMENT PLANS**

**Subscription Plans:**

* 97 total configuration options
* Include trial day variations
* Recurring billing models with ongoing access
* More granular configuration combinations
* Focused on continuous service delivery

**Payment Plans:**

* 49 total configuration options
* No trial day options
* One-time or installment-based payments
* Simpler configuration structure
* Focused on defined payment schedules

\---

## **USE CASES**

**Multi-Language Sites:** Customise pricing terminology for different languages and regions

**Brand Consistency:** Override default text with brand-specific pricing language

**Regional Compliance:** Adapt pricing text to meet local regulatory requirements

**A/B Testing:** Test different pricing language to optimise conversions

**Industry-Specific Terminology:** Use domain-specific payment terms (e.g., tuition, membership fees, subscriptions)

**White-Labeling:** Replace default Zenler terminology with custom branded language

\---

## **TOTAL PRICING CONFIGURATIONS**

**Subscription Plan Details:** 97 options  
**Payment Plan Details:** 49 options  
**Grand Total:** 146 distinct pricing configuration options

Each option allows customisation of all text strings that appear when that specific pricing scenario is displayed to users throughout the platform.

## **SITE → COUPONS - CONSOLIDATED OVERVIEW**

### **NAVIGATION \& ACCESS**

**Location:** Site / Coupons  
**Left Sidebar Position:** Between Language and Gamification  
**Primary Actions:**

* Add Coupon (creation)
* Edit Coupon (modification)
* Delete Coupon (removal)
* Get URL (sharing)

\---

## **COUPON TYPES**

### **Single Coupon**

* Creates one unique code
* Manual entry or auto-generation
* Individual tracking
* One-to-one or broadcast distribution

### **Bulk Coupon**

* Generates multiple unique codes from single creation
* Prefix-based generation system
* Shared discount settings across all codes
* Individual redemption tracking per code
* Mass distribution capability

\---

## **CORE CONFIGURATION FIELDS**

### **Coupon Identity**

**Coupon Title:** Internal reference name (optional)  
**Coupon Code:** Alphanumeric identifier (Single only)  
**Prefix:** Code prefix for bulk generation (Bulk only)  
**No. of Coupons:** Quantity to generate (Bulk only)  
**Description:** Optional notes/context

### **Code Generation Options**

* Manual entry (user types code)
* Auto-generate random code
* Prefix + identifier system (bulk)
* Alphanumeric validation only
* Uniqueness checking

\---

## **DISCOUNT CONFIGURATION**

### **Discount Types**

**Percentage:**

* 0-100% range
* Applies to product price
* Can apply to all products or specific products
* No currency restrictions

**Amount:**

* Fixed monetary value
* Must apply to specific products only
* All selected products must share same currency
* Cannot exceed product price

### **Discount Value Fields**

**Percentage Display:** Number input + "%" symbol  
**Amount Display:** Currency symbol + number input + ".00"  
**Validation:** Positive values required

\---

## **APPLICABILITY OPTIONS**

### **Product Scope**

* **All Products:** Courses, bundles, live classes, live webinars (percentage only)
* **All Courses:** Course products only
* **All Live Class:** Live class offerings only
* **All Live Webinars:** Live webinar offerings only
* **Specific Products:** Selective application (required for amount discounts)

### **Applicability Rules**

* Percentage discounts: can apply to all or specific
* Amount discounts: must apply to specific products only
* Currency matching enforced for amount discounts
* Multi-select interface for specific products

\---

## **USAGE LIMITATIONS**

### **Redemption Limit**

* Optional field (blank = unlimited)
* Positive integer validation
* Tracks total uses across all users
* Cannot be reduced below current usage count when editing

### **Expiry Date**

* Optional date picker (blank = no expiry)
* Calendar interface with month/year navigation
* Coupon deactivates at end of selected date
* Must be future date
* Can be modified or removed when editing

\---

## **RECURRING DISCOUNT OPTIONS**

### **Availability**

* Subscription and payment plan products only
* Not applicable to one-time purchases

### **Billing Options**

**Apply discount during every billing:**

* Continues indefinitely until subscription ends
* Compatible with all payment gateways including PayPal

**Apply discount for the first billing only:**

* Initial payment only, full price thereafter
* Not compatible with PayPal recurring plans

**Apply discount for \[X] months:**

* Custom duration via dropdown
* Regular pricing resumes after period
* Not compatible with PayPal recurring plans

### **PayPal Limitation**

Only "every billing" option supported for PayPal recurring plans. Modal warning appears when incompatible options selected.

\---

## **COUPON MANAGEMENT INTERFACE**

### **List View Features**

**Search Functionality:**

* Search by coupon code
* Search by title/name
* Search by description text
* Real-time filtering

**Column Customisation:**

* "More Fields" dropdown
* Show/hide columns
* Optimise table width
* User-customisable view

**Default Visible Columns:**

* Title
* Type (Single/Bulk)
* Coupon Code
* Description
* Discount Type
* Discount (value + "off")
* Redemption Limit (with usage count)
* Expiry Date
* Applicable Product(s)
* Actions

**Results Counter:** "Showing X Results" displays active count

\---

## **ROW-LEVEL ACTIONS**

### **Action Icons (per coupon)**

**Get URL:** Copy shareable coupon link

* Format: `https://\\\\\\\[domain]/checkout?coupon=\\\\\\\[CODE]`
* Click to copy to clipboard
* Distribution via email, social media, landing pages

**Edit:** Modify existing coupon

* Opens Edit Coupon modal
* Limited field editing (description, limits, expiry, products, recurring terms)
* Core fields immutable (code, discount type, discount value)

**Delete:** Remove coupon

* Triggers confirmation modal (assumed)
* Permanent removal
* Use with caution for active campaigns

\---

## **DATA DISPLAY FORMATS**

### **Redemption Limit Display**

* **Unlimited:** "Unlimited (X used)"
* **Limited:** "X (Y used)" format
* Real-time usage tracking
* Visual indicators when limit approached/reached

### **Discount Display**

* **Percentage:** "XX% off"
* **Amount:** "$XX.XX off" (currency-based)
* Consistent "off" suffix

### **Type Indicator**

* **Single:** Individual one-code coupon
* **Bulk:** Prefix-based batch

### **Empty Fields**

* Blank cells indicate optional fields not set
* No expiry = unlimited validity
* No applicable products shown = all products
* No title = internal reference not set

\---

## **EDITING RESTRICTIONS**

### **Immutable Fields (Cannot Edit)**

* Coupon code
* Discount type (percentage/amount)
* Discount value (percentage or amount)

**Rationale:**

* Maintains tracking integrity
* Preserves external link validity
* Ensures historical accuracy
* Protects financial reconciliation

**Solution:** Delete and recreate coupon to change core parameters

### **Editable Fields**

* Description
* Redemption limit (cannot reduce below current usage)
* Expiry date (must be future date)
* Applicable products and plans
* Recurring discount billing options

\---

## **VALIDATION RULES**

### **Code Validation**

* Letters and numbers only
* No spaces or special characters
* Must be unique across platform
* Case sensitivity (platform-dependent)

### **Discount Constraints**

* Percentage: 0-100% range
* Amount: positive value greater than 0
* Amount discounts require specific product selection
* Currency matching for amount discounts

### **Date Validation**

* Expiry must be future date
* Cannot set past dates
* Optional field

### **Limit Validation**

* Positive integers only if specified
* Cannot be less than current usage count
* Blank = unlimited uses

\---

## **USE CASE CATEGORIES**

### **Promotional Campaigns**

* Seasonal sales
* Holiday promotions
* Launch discounts
* Flash sales

### **Customer Acquisition**

* First-time buyer incentives
* Email capture offers
* Social media follower rewards
* Trial incentives

### **Customer Retention**

* Loyalty rewards
* Win-back campaigns
* Renewal incentives
* Subscriber discounts

### **VIP \& Exclusive Access**

* Early bird pricing
* Member-only discounts
* Beta tester rewards
* Insider offers

### **Partnership \& Distribution**

* Affiliate codes
* Corporate partnerships
* Cross-promotions
* Conference giveaways

### **Campaign Tracking**

* Multi-channel attribution (bulk prefixes)
* Regional campaigns
* Time-based batches
* Performance monitoring

\---

## **WORKFLOW SUMMARY**

### **Create Single Coupon**

Navigate → Add Coupon → Single tab → Enter title/code → Select discount type/value → Choose applicability → Set limits/expiry → Configure recurring options → Add

### **Create Bulk Coupon**

Navigate → Add Coupon → Bulk tab → Enter title/prefix/quantity → Select discount type/value → Choose applicability → Set expiry → Configure recurring options → Add → System generates all codes

### **View \& Search**

Navigate → View list → Use search bar → Filter by keyword → Customise columns → Review results

### **Get Shareable URL**

Locate coupon → Click link icon → Copy URL → Distribute via channels

### **Edit Existing**

Locate coupon → Click edit icon → Modify editable fields → Update → Changes saved

### **Delete Coupon**

Locate coupon → Click delete icon → Confirm deletion → Coupon removed

\---

## **SYSTEM FEATURES**

### **Tracking Capabilities**

* Individual redemption counting
* Usage vs limit monitoring
* Real-time usage updates
* Historical accuracy preservation

### **URL Generation**

* Automatic checkout link creation
* Pre-applied coupon parameter
* Shareable distribution format
* Direct purchase path

### **Batch Management**

* Multiple codes from single creation
* Shared settings across batch
* Individual tracking per code
* Prefix-based organisation

### **Column Customisation**

* Show/hide table columns
* Optimise display width
* User preference retention
* Workflow adaptation

### **Search \& Filter**

* Multi-field search capability
* Real-time results filtering
* Results counter display
* Clear search function

\---

## **COMPATIBILITY \& RESTRICTIONS**

### **Payment Gateway Compatibility**

**All Gateways:**

* All recurring discount options supported (except PayPal limitations)

**PayPal Recurring:**

* Only "every billing" option compatible
* "First billing only" not supported
* "Custom months" not supported
* Modal warning displays when incompatible options selected

### **Product Type Compatibility**

**One-Time Purchases:**

* Percentage and amount discounts available
* No recurring options

**Subscriptions/Payment Plans:**

* Percentage and amount discounts available
* Recurring discount options available
* PayPal limitations apply

### **Currency Requirements**

**Percentage Discounts:**

* No currency restrictions
* Works across all currencies

**Amount Discounts:**

* Must select specific products only
* All selected products must use same currency
* Currency validation enforced

\---

## **KEY SYSTEM LOGIC**

### **Code Uniqueness**

Platform-wide uniqueness checking prevents duplicate codes across all coupons

### **Redemption Tracking**

Real-time usage counting tracks each redemption against set limits

### **Expiry Management**

System automatically deactivates coupons at end of selected expiry date

### **Validation Enforcement**

Field-level validation prevents invalid configurations before submission

### **Edit Protection**

Core parameters locked after creation to maintain integrity and prevent tracking conflicts

### **Bulk Generation**

Prefix + identifier system creates specified quantity of unique codes with shared settings

## **EU VAT SETTINGS**

### **Payment Responsibility**

**Question:** "Do you want zenler to pay EU VAT for you?"

**Description:** "You can choose either zenler to pay EU VAT on behalf of you, or you can pay yourself. In both cases, a detailed report of purchase including tax will be shown to you."

**Options (Radio buttons):**

* ⚫ "You will be responsible for filing and remitting tax to respective EU governments."
* ⚪ "Zenler will report and pay the tax on behalf of you"

### **VAT ID Collection**

**Question:** "Allow EU/UK businesses to add VAT ID on the checkout page?"

**Description:** "Tax can be exempted if a valid VAT ID is submitted during checkout."

**Control:**

* Toggle switch: "Add VAT ID"
* Function: Enable/disable VAT ID field at checkout

\---

## **ADDITIONAL TAX SETTINGS**

### **Tax Calculation Method**

**Question:** "Please choose whether the tax is inclusive or exclusive of course price"

**Description:** "If you choose tax to add on top of price, the tax will be calculated and added to the price to arrive at the total payable amount. If you choose tax included in price, the tax will be included as part of the price."

**Options (Radio buttons):**

* ⚫ "Add Tax on top of price"
* ⚪ "Tax included in price"

\---

**Example Value:** "VAT"

**Close:** X button (top right)

* Dropdown

  * ☐ Physical / Services

## **STATES SECTION (US ONLY)**

### **Visibility**

Appears when "United States" selected as country

### **Section Header**

**Heading:** "States"  
**Description:** "Add states you'd like to charge taxes."

### **Add State Tax**

**Button:** "Add State Tax" (outlined purple, centred)  
**Function:** Adds new state tax row to table

### **State Tax Table**

**Columns:**

* State
* Tax Name
* Tax Rate
* Option
* Action

**Row Fields:**

**State Column:**

* Dropdown
* Example: "Alabama"

**Tax Name Column:**

* Text input
* Custom name for state tax

**Tax Rate Column:**

* Number input
* Example: "4.00"
* Decimal format

**Option Column:**

* Dropdown with two options:

  * "Ignore Country Tax" (state tax only)
  * "Add to Country Tax" (combined calculation)
* Blue checkmark shows selected option

**Action Column:**

* Purple checkmark icon (confirm row)
* X icon (remove row)

**Multiple States:** Can add multiple state rows by clicking "Add State Tax" repeatedly

\---

## **FORM ACTIONS**

**Tax Settings Page:**

* Button: "Update" (purple solid, bottom right)
* Saves all tax settings changes

**Edit Tax Name Modal:**

* Button: "Save" (purple solid, bottom right)
* Saves name change and closes modal

**Add New Tax Rate Modal:**

* Button: "Save" (purple solid, bottom right)
* Saves tax rate configuration and closes modal

\---

## **TAX CALCULATION OPTIONS**

### **Add Tax on Top of Price**

Tax calculated and added to product price to arrive at total payable amount

### **Tax Included in Price**

Tax included as part of displayed product price

\---

## **STATE TAX CALCULATION OPTIONS**

### **Ignore Country Tax**

* State tax applied independently
* Country/federal tax not included
* Only state tax charged

### **Add to Country Tax**

* State tax added to country/federal tax
* Combined tax calculation
* Total = Country Tax + State Tax

\---

## **EU VAT PAYMENT OPTIONS**

### **Seller-Managed**

"You will be responsible for filing and remitting tax to respective EU governments."

* Seller handles all EU VAT compliance
* Detailed purchase report provided

### **Zenler-Managed**

"Zenler will report and pay the tax on behalf of you"

* Zenler handles EU VAT compliance
* Detailed purchase report provided

\---

## **VAT ID FUNCTIONALITY**

**Purpose:** Allow EU/UK business customers to provide VAT ID at checkout  
**Behaviour:** Valid VAT ID exempts transaction from tax  
**Control:** Toggle switch enables/disables VAT ID field on checkout page

\---

## **TAX CATEGORY OPTIONS**

**Digital:**

* Applies tax to digital products/services

**Physical / Services:**

* Applies tax to physical goods and services

**Selection:** Both can be selected simultaneously or independently

\---

## **COUNTRY-SPECIFIC BEHAVIOUR**

### **Non-US Countries**

* Country dropdown
* Country Tax Rate
* Tax Name
* Tax Category checkboxes
* No States section

### **United States**

* Country dropdown
* Country Tax Rate
* Tax Name
* Tax Category checkboxes
* States section visible
* "Add State Tax" button
* State tax table when states added

## **WORKFLOW SUMMARY**

### **Add Basic Tax Rate (Non-US)**

Navigate → Click "Add New Tax Rate" → Select Country → Enter Country Tax Rate → Enter Tax Name → Select Tax Category → Save

### **Add US Tax Rate with States**

Navigate → Click "Add New Tax Rate" → Select "United States" → Enter Country Tax Rate → Enter Tax Name → Select Tax Category → Click "Add State Tax" → Select State → Enter Tax Name → Enter Tax Rate → Select Option (Ignore/Add to Country Tax) → Click checkmark → Repeat for additional states → Save

### **Edit Tax Name**

Locate tax in table → Click pencil icon → Modify name in text field → Save

### **Enable/Disable Tax Rate**

Locate tax in table → Click toggle switch in Action column → Status updates

### **Configure EU VAT Settings**

Select payment responsibility option (radio button) → Toggle VAT ID collection (on/off) → Click "Update"

### **Set Tax Calculation Method**

Select "Add Tax on top of price" or "Tax included in price" (radio button) → Click "Update"

### **Remove State Tax Row**

Locate state row in table → Click X icon in Action column → Row removed

### **EDIT LEVEL MODAL**

#### **Modal Trigger**

Click "Edit" link next to any level

#### **Modal Structure**

**Title:** "Edit level"  
**Close:** X button (top right)  
**Background:** White modal on greyed overlay

#### **Form Fields**

**1. Name**

* Label: "Name"
* Field Type: Text input
* Examples: "Level 1", "Level 2", "Level 3", "Level 4"

**2. Points Required**

* Label: "Points Required"
* Field Type: Number input
* Examples: Empty (Level 1), "75", "150", "300"

**3. Level Unlock**

* Label: "Level Unlock"
* Field Type: Dropdown
* Default: "None"
* Options:

  * None
  * Course
  * Community

**4. Select Product** (Conditional Field)

* Label: "Select Product"
* Field Type: Dropdown
* Visibility: Appears only when "Course" or "Community" selected in Level Unlock
* Default: "Select Product" placeholder
* Purpose: Choose specific course or community to unlock

**Conditional Logic:**

* If Level Unlock = "None" → Select Product hidden
* If Level Unlock = "Course" → Select Product shows available courses
* If Level Unlock = "Community" → Select Product shows available communities

#### **Modal Action**

**Apply Button:** Purple solid button (bottom right)

\---

#### **Section Header**

**Heading:** "Point System Configuration"

#### **Point Activities List**

**Course Enrollment**

* Description: "Users earn points when they enroll in a course on the platform."
* Default Points: 25

**Purchase**

* Description: "Users earn points when they complete their initial purchase on the platform."
* Default Points: 75

**Lesson Completion**

* Description: "Users earn points when they complete a single lesson on the platform."
* Default Points: 5

**Assignment Submission**

* Description: "Users earn points when they submit an assignment on the platform."
* Default Points: 10

**Course Completion**

* Description: "Users earn points when they complete an entire course on the platform."
* Default Points: 50

**Community: Post Like**

* Description: "Users earn points when their post receives likes."
* Default Points: 1

**Community: Post Comment**

* Description: "Users earn points when their post receives comments."
* Default Points: 2

**7-Day Streak**

* Description: "Users earn points when they maintain 7 consecutive days of activity on the platform."
* Default Points: 50

**Apply Button:** Purple solid button (bottom right)

|Activity|Default Points|

|Course Completion|50|
|Post Like|1|
|Post Comment|2|
|7-Day Streak|50|

#### **Unlockable Features**

* None (no unlock)
* Course (specific course unlocked)
* Community (specific community unlocked)

#### **Celebration System**

* 6 animation options
* Customizable celebration text (4 fields)
* Random animation option
* Preview functionality

#### **Leaderboard System**

* Three time periods (7 days, 30 days, all time)
* Top 10 rankings per period
* Medal/trophy icons for top 3
* User avatars and names
* Point totals displayed
* Empty state messaging

\---

### **WORKFLOW SUMMARY**

#### **Enable Gamification**

Navigate → Toggle "Enable Gamification" switch → System activated

#### **Configure Level**

Navigate → Level Configuration tab → Click "Edit" on desired level → Modify Name → Set Points Required → Select Level Unlock option → (If Course/Community selected) Choose specific product from Select Product dropdown → Click "Apply"

#### **Adjust Point Values**

Navigate → Point Configuration tab → Review default point values for activities → Modify values as needed → Click "Apply" or "Reset" to restore defaults

#### **Customize Celebration**

Navigate → Celebration tab → Click "Change Animation" → Select animation style (or enable random) → Preview if desired → Click "Apply" → Customize text fields (Level Up Text, Celebration Text, Level Unlock Text, Button Text) → Click "Update"

#### **View Leaderboards**

Navigate → Leaderboard tab → View rankings across three time periods (7 Days, 30 Days, All time) → Top 10 users displayed per period

#### **Reset Gamification**

Navigate → Hover over "Reset Gamification" link (top right) → Tooltip displays "Reset Points, Levels And Streak For All Users In The School" → Click to confirm → All user progress reset

\---

### **CONFIGURATION OPTIONS**

#### **Level Configuration Options**

* Customize level names
* Set progressive point thresholds
* Assign unlockable content (courses or communities)
* Track user distribution across levels

#### **Point Configuration Options**

* Adjust point values for 8 activities
* Balance engagement incentives
* Reset to default values
* Apply custom point economy

#### **Celebration Configuration Options**

* Choose from 6 animation styles
* Enable random animation selection
* Customize 4 text fields
* Preview animations before applying
* Maintain default celebration option

#### **Leaderboard Configuration Options**

* Three automatic time periods
* Top 10 display per period
* Visual ranking indicators
* Reset all user progress option

### **COMPREHENSIVE WORKFLOWS**

#### **Upload File from Computer**

1. Navigate to Media Library
2. Click "Upload Files" button
3. Modal opens with "My Device" tab active
4. Click upload area or drag files
5. Select file(s) from computer
6. Files upload with progress indicator
7. Files appear in Media Library table

#### **Upload File from Cloud Storage**

1. Click "Upload Files" button
2. Select cloud storage tab (Dropbox, Google Drive, Box, OneDrive, OneDrive Business)
3. Authenticate with cloud service (if first time)
4. Grant permissions
5. Browse cloud files
6. Select file(s) to upload
7. Files transfer to Media Library
8. Files appear in table

#### **Upload SCORM/HTML Package**

1. Click "Upload SCORM/HTML" button
2. Modal opens
3. Enter Title
4. Drag/drop ZIP file or click to browse
5. Click "Start Upload"
6. Package uploads and processes
7. Package appears in Media Library

#### **Search for Files**

1. Enter filename in "Find file" search box
2. Results filter in real-time
3. Clear search to show all files

#### **Filter by File Type**

1. Click "Select File Type" dropdown
2. Choose type from list (PDF, PPT, Excel, Doc, Image, Video, Audio, Text, SCORM/HTML, Other)
3. Table filters to show only selected type
4. Reset by selecting "Select File Type"

#### **Filter by Tag**

1. Click "Select Tag" dropdown
2. Choose tag from list
3. Table filters to show tagged files
4. Reset by deselecting tag

#### **Edit File Details (Image/PDF)**

1. Locate file in table
2. Click Edit (pencil) icon in Actions
3. Edit page opens
4. Modify Title field
5. Select tags from dropdown
6. Click "Update" to save changes

#### **Edit Video File**

1. Locate video in table
2. Click Edit icon
3. Edit page opens with video-specific sections
4. Modify Title and Tags in Settings
5. Add/manage closed captions in Closed Captions section
6. Update thumbnail in Video Thumbnail section
7. Click "Update" to save

#### **Replace File**

1. Open file edit page
2. Click "Replace File" button
3. File browser opens
4. Select new file
5. New file uploads and replaces original
6. URL and metadata remain unchanged

#### **Update Video Thumbnail (Frame Capture)**

1. Open video edit page
2. Click "Update Thumbnail" button
3. Modal opens with video player
4. Play video to desired frame
5. Pause at target moment
6. Click "Select Current Video Frame"
7. Thumbnail updates automatically
8. Modal closes

#### **Update Video Thumbnail (Custom Upload)**

1. Open video edit page
2. Click "Update Thumbnail" button
3. Modal opens
4. Click "Upload Custom Thumbnail"
5. Select image from computer
6. Image uploads as thumbnail
7. Thumbnail updates automatically

#### **Add Closed Captions to Video**

1. Open video edit page
2. Scroll to Closed Captions section
3. Click "Add Closed Captions" button
4. Modal opens
5. Select language from dropdown
6. Upload .vtt or .srt file (drag/drop or browse)
7. File uploads (up to 2GB)
8. Caption appears in Closed Captions table with Language, CC File Name, Status, Action

#### **Preview File**

1. Locate file in table
2. Click View (eye) icon in Actions
3. Preview opens in modal or new window
4. Close preview when done

#### **Share File (Standard)**

1. Open file edit page or click Share icon in table
2. Share modal opens
3. Select permission level:

   * Logged in users (requires authentication)
   * Anyone with link (public)
4. Copy download link using copy button
5. Share link via email, messaging, etc.
6. Click "Done" to close

#### **Share Video with Embed Code**

1. Open video edit page
2. Click "Share" button
3. Modal opens with video-specific fields
4. Scroll to "Video embed code"
5. Copy iframe code using copy button
6. Paste in external website HTML
7. Video player embeds on site
8. Click "Done"

#### **Share Video Player Link**

1. Open video file Share modal
2. Locate "Video player link" field
3. Click copy button (link icon)
4. Share direct player URL
5. Recipients access video in Zenler player

#### **Download File**

1. Locate file in table
2. Click Download (arrow) icon in Actions
3. File downloads to computer
4. Check downloads folder

#### **Delete File**

1. Locate file in table
2. Click Delete (trash) icon in Actions
3. Confirmation prompt appears
4. Confirm deletion
5. File removed from library

#### **View Video Analytics**

1. Locate video file in table
2. Click Analytics (chart) icon in Actions
3. Analytics page/modal opens
4. View video statistics:

   * Views
   * Watch time
   * Engagement metrics
   * Viewer data

#### **Bulk Select and Action**

1. Check multiple file checkboxes
2. Selection counter updates: "X Media(s) Selected"
3. Bulk Actions button appears (top right)
4. Click "Bulk Actions" dropdown
5. Select action (delete, tag, move, etc.)
6. Confirm action
7. Action applies to all selected files

#### **Clear Bulk Selection**

1. Select multiple files
2. Click "Clear selection" link
3. All checkboxes deselect
4. Bulk Actions button disappears

#### **STEP 1: UPLOAD**

* Next button: "Next >" (purple solid)

#### **STEP 2: MAP COLUMNS**

**Heading:** "2. Map Columns"

**Function:** Maps CSV columns to Zenler user fields

**Field Mapping Interface:**

* CSV Column Name → Zenler Field dropdown
* Automatic mapping for standard fields
* Manual override capability
* Required field indicators

**Standard Mappings:**

* First Name → First Name
* Last Name → Last Name
* Email → Email
* Phone → Phone
* Address → Address
* City → City
* State → State
* Country → Country
* Zip Code → Zip Code
* Tags → Tags

**Navigation:**

* Previous button: "< Previous" (active)
* Next button: "Next >" (purple solid)

\---

#### **STEP 3: ACTIONS**

**Heading:** "3. Actions"

**Function:** Select actions to perform on imported users

**Available Actions:**

* Send welcome email
* Enroll in specific course
* Subscribe to funnel
* Add to community
* Register for live event
* Apply specific tags

**Action Configuration:**

* Checkboxes for each action
* Conditional fields based on selection
* Course/funnel/community selectors

**Navigation:**

* Previous button: "< Previous" (active)
* Next button: "Next >" (purple solid)

\---

#### **STEP 4: FINISH**

**Heading:** "4. Finish"

**Function:** Review and confirm import

**Review Display:**

* Total users to import
* Selected role
* Actions to perform
* Warnings or conflicts

**Import Actions:**

* Previous button: "< Previous" (active)
* Finish button: "Finish" (purple solid)

**Post-Import:**

* Success message
* Import summary
* Users added to table

### **USER ROLES**

#### **Available Roles**

**Site Admin**

* Full administrative access
* Can manage all site settings
* Access to all users and data
* Can create/edit/delete content

**Instructor**

* Can create and manage courses
* Can access student data for their courses
* Can manage discussions and assignments
* Limited admin capabilities

**Student**

* Basic user role
* Can enroll in courses and access content
* Can participate in discussions
* Can view own progress

**Affiliate**

* Can promote courses
* Earns commissions on referrals
* Access to affiliate dashboard
* Tracking and reporting tools

**Lead**

* Potential customer
* Limited access, typically pre-enrollment
* Can browse public content
* Conversion tracking

**Assistant**

* Support role with limited permissions
* Can assist instructors
* Can access specific courses/users
* No full admin access

**Support**

* Customer support access
* Can view user issues
* Can respond to tickets
* Limited editing capabilities

**Unverified**

* New users pending verification
* Email verification required
* Limited access until verified
* Automatic role assignment

\---

#### **Tag System**

**Purpose:** Organize and segment users  
**Format:** Comma-separated labels, typically uppercase

* LIFETIME ACCESS

* Filtering and segmentation
* Automation triggers
* Reporting and analytics
* Email targeting
* Access control

### **COMPREHENSIVE WORKFLOWS**

#### **Add Single User**

1. Navigate to Site → People → All
2. Click "Add User" button
3. Fill required fields: First Name, Last Name, E-Mail Address
4. Select Role(s) from dropdown
5. (Optional) Click "Set Manual Password"
6. (Optional) Select Tags
7. (Optional) Fill address fields
8. (Optional) Check "Send a Notification Email"
9. Click "Add" button
10. User created and appears in People table

#### **Import Multiple Users via CSV**

1. Click "Import Users" button
2. Step 1: Select role (default Student), click "Choose file"
3. Upload CSV file (download sample if needed)
4. Click "Next >"
5. Step 2: Map CSV columns to Zenler fields
6. Verify mappings, click "Next >"
7. Step 3: Select post-import actions (enroll, subscribe, etc.)
8. Click "Next >"
9. Step 4: Review import summary
10. Click "Finish"
11. Users imported and added to table

#### **Search for User**

1. Enter name or email in search box
2. Results filter in real-time
3. Review filtered results
4. Clear search to restore full list

#### **Filter by Role (Sidebar)**

1. Click role category in left sidebar (Site Admin, Instructor, Student, etc.)
2. Table filters to show only that role
3. Results counter updates
4. Click "All" to restore full list

#### **Use Advanced Filter**

1. Click "Advanced Filter" dropdown
2. Select filter criteria (Joined Date, Role, Tags, Course enrollment, etc.)
3. Configure filter parameters
4. Click "+ Add Filter" for multiple criteria
5. Click "Apply Filter"
6. Table displays filtered results
7. Click X to clear filters

#### **Edit User Profile**

1. Locate user in table
2. Click Edit (pencil) icon OR click user's name
3. Profile page opens on Profile tab
4. Modify desired fields
5. Click "Update" button
6. Changes saved

#### **Change User Roles**

1. Open user profile
2. Locate "Role" field with badge display
3. Click X on role badge to remove
4. Select new roles from dropdown
5. Click "Update"
6. Roles updated

#### **Manage User Tags**

1. Open user profile
2. Locate "Tags" field
3. Click X on tag badge to remove
4. Add new tags via dropdown
5. Click "Update"
6. Tags saved

#### **Change User Password**

1. Open user profile
2. Enter new password in "Password" field
3. Click "Update"
4. Password changed

#### **Update User Address**

1. Open user profile
2. Modify Address, City, State, Country, Zip Code fields
3. Click "Update"
4. Address information saved

#### **Update Profile Photo**

1. Open user profile (Profile tab)
2. Hover over profile photo
3. Click "Change" button
4. Select image file (200x200px recommended)
5. Image uploads and displays

#### **Remove Profile Photo**

1. Open user profile (Profile tab)
2. Click "Remove" button below photo
3. Confirm removal
4. Photo deleted, default avatar shows

#### **View Activity History**

1. Open user profile
2. Activities panel displays on right sidebar
3. Review chronological activity list
4. Click linked items for details
5. Scroll for full history

#### **Manage Email Preferences**

1. Open user profile
2. Click info icon (ℹ) next to "Email subscription status"
3. Email Preferences modal opens
4. Check/uncheck categories: Courses, List, Email Broadcasts, Community
5. Expand categories for subcategory options
6. Click "Update Preferences"
7. Preferences saved

#### **Unsubscribe User from All Emails**

1. Open Email Preferences modal
2. Click "Unsubscribe from All Emails" button
3. Confirm unsubscribe
4. All email subscriptions disabled
5. Status changes to "Unsubscribed"

#### **Update Privacy Consent**

1. Navigate to Privacy tab
2. Check or uncheck "Marketing consent status"
3. Click "Update"
4. Consent status saved

#### **Manually Enroll User in Course**

1. Navigate to Courses tab
2. Click "Select a course to enroll" dropdown
3. Search/select course
4. (Optional) Select plan
5. Leave notification checkbox checked (or uncheck)
6. Click "Enroll" button
7. User enrolled, course appears in table

#### **View Course Progress**

1. Open Courses tab
2. Review "Enrolled courses" table
3. Check Progress column for completion percentage
4. Click "View details" for detailed progress

#### **Unenroll User from Course**

1. Locate course in Enrolled courses table
2. Click "Unenroll" button
3. Confirm unenrollment
4. User removed from course

#### **View Purchase History**

1. Navigate to Purchases tab
2. Review overview cards (Total Purchases, Total Paid Amount)
3. Check Purchased Products table
4. Click "View details" for transaction details

#### **Add User to Community**

1. Navigate to Communities tab
2. Click "Select Community or Categories" dropdown
3. Select community
4. Click "Add" button
5. User added to community
6. Community appears in list

#### **Remove User from Community**

1. Locate community in Communities table
2. Click delete (trash) icon
3. Confirm removal
4. User removed from community

#### **Grant Discussion Access**

1. Navigate to Discussions tab
2. Click "Select Course" dropdown
3. Choose course
4. Click "Add" button
5. User granted discussion access
6. Course appears in Lesson Discussions table

#### **Remove Discussion Access**

1. Locate course in Lesson Discussions table
2. Click delete icon
3. Confirm removal
4. Discussion access revoked

#### **Subscribe User to Funnel**

1. Navigate to Funnels tab
2. Click "Subscribe to Funnel" dropdown
3. Select funnel
4. Click "Subscribe" button
5. User subscribed
6. Funnel appears in Subscribed Funnels table

#### **Unsubscribe User from Funnel**

1. Locate funnel in Subscribed Funnels table
2. Click "Unsubscribe" button
3. Confirm unsubscription
4. User removed from funnel

#### **Register User for Live Event**

1. Navigate to Live tab
2. Select event type from dropdown (Live Class, 1-1 Booking, etc.)
3. Click "Select" dropdown
4. Choose specific event
5. Click "Register" button
6. User registered
7. Registration appears in table

#### **Add Note to User Profile**

1. Navigate to Notes tab
2. Click "📄 Add New Note" button
3. Enter Title
4. Enter Description with formatting
5. Click "Save"
6. Note saved and appears in list

#### **Send Individual Email to User**

1. Locate user in table
2. Click Send Email (envelope) icon
3. Send Email modal opens
4. Review From Name and From Email (pre-filled)
5. Enter Subject
6. Compose email using rich text editor
7. (Optional) Click "Personalize" to add merge tags
8. (Optional) Send test email
9. Click "Send" button
10. Email sent to user

#### **Bulk Select Users**

1. Check individual user checkboxes OR
2. Check "Select all" in header
3. Selection counter updates
4. Click "Select all X Users" link for all pages
5. Proceed with bulk action

#### **Perform Bulk Action**

1. Select multiple users
2. Click "Bulk Actions" dropdown
3. Select action (Enroll in Course, Add Tag, Delete, etc.)
4. Configure action parameters
5. Confirm action
6. Action applies to all selected users

#### **Toggle Column Visibility**

1. Click "More Fields" dropdown
2. Check boxes for columns to show
3. Uncheck boxes for columns to hide
4. Table updates immediately
5. Custom view maintained

#### **Export Users to CSV**

1. (Optional) Apply filters or search
2. Click "Export(CSV)" button
3. "Export in Progress" modal appears
4. Click "OK"
5. CSV generates in background
6. Email sent with download link
7. Download CSV from email

#### **Delete User**

1. Locate user in table
2. Click Delete (trash) icon
3. Confirmation prompt appears: "Are you sure you want to delete this user?"
4. Click "Confirm"
5. User deleted from system
6. All associated data removed

#### **Cancel Profile Edit**

1. Make changes to user profile
2. Click "Cancel" button
3. Confirmation prompt: "Discard changes?"
4. Click "Yes"
5. Changes discarded
6. Return to People list

\---

### **KEY FEATURES SUMMARY**

#### **User Management**

✓ Add individual users manually  
✓ Import users in bulk via CSV  
✓ Edit user profiles and details  
✓ Delete users with confirmation  
✓ Assign roles (8 role types)  
✓ Tag users for organization  
✓ Search by name or email (real-time)  
✓ Advanced filtering (40+ criteria)  
✓ 55,793+ user capacity (scalable)

#### **Communication**

✓ Send individual emails with rich text editor  
✓ Email personalization with merge tags  
✓ Send test emails before sending  
✓ Pre-filled sender information  
✓ Track email subscription status  
✓ Granular email preference controls  
✓ One-click unsubscribe from all

#### **Bulk Operations**

✓ Bulk select (page or all users)  
✓ Bulk enroll/unenroll in courses  
✓ Bulk subscribe/unsubscribe to funnels  
✓ Bulk register/unregister for classes  
✓ Bulk tag management (add/remove)  
✓ Bulk delete with confirmation  
✓ Bulk export to CSV with email delivery

#### **Profile Management**

✓ 9 profile tabs (Profile, Privacy, Courses, Purchases, Communities, Discussions, Funnels, Live, Notes)  
✓ Profile photo upload (200x200px recommended)  
✓ Multiple role assignment per user  
✓ Multi-tag management with visual badges  
✓ Password reset capability  
✓ Complete address management  
✓ Activity history tracking

#### **Course Management**

✓ Manual course enrollment  
✓ Plan-based or plan-free enrollment  
✓ Enrollment notification toggle  
✓ Visual progress tracking with bars  
✓ Percentage completion display  
✓ Quick unenroll functionality  
✓ Detailed enrollment view

#### **Advanced Features**

✓ Community membership management  
✓ Discussion access control per course  
✓ Funnel subscription management  
✓ Live event registration (4 types)  
✓ Purchase history tracking  
✓ Admin notes with rich formatting  
✓ Privacy consent tracking  
✓ Email preference granularity

#### **Data Management**

✓ 15 users per page (default pagination)  
✓ Export to CSV with email delivery  
✓ Custom field visibility controls  
✓ Address and contact information storage  
✓ Join date tracking (DD MMM YYYY)  
✓ Historical data from 2019+

#### **Filtering \& Search**

✓ Real-time search by name/email  
✓ 40+ advanced filter criteria  
✓ Role-based sidebar filtering (9 roles)  
✓ Tag filtering  
✓ Course enrollment filtering  
✓ Email engagement filtering (opened, clicked, bounced)  
✓ Live event attendance filtering  
✓ Custom field filtering  
✓ Combined filter support

#### **Import System**

✓ 4-step import wizard  
✓ CSV file upload with sample template  
✓ Column mapping interface  
✓ Bulk actions on import  
✓ Role-specific import  
✓ Import validation and error handling

#### **Reporting \& Analytics**

✓ Total users count  
✓ Results counter with filtering  
✓ Activity history per user  
✓ Purchase totals display  
✓ Course completion tracking  
✓ Email subscription status  
✓ Export capabilities for external analysis

\---

### **TECHNICAL SPECIFICATIONS**

#### **User Capacity**

* Scalable to 55,793+ users (as shown in example)
* No hard limits on user count
* Performance optimized for large databases

#### **Data Fields**

* **Required:** First Name, Last Name, Email
* **Optional:** Address, City, State, Country, Zip Code, Phone, Tags
* **System:** Join Date, Role(s), Email Status, Activity History

#### **File Formats**

* **Import:** CSV
* **Export:** CSV
* **Sample Files:** Downloadable CSV templates

#### **Email Capabilities**

* Rich text editor with full formatting
* Merge tags for personalization
* Test email functionality
* Individual and bulk sending
* Subscription management

#### **Security**

* Password management
* Role-based access control
* Email verification for new users
* Privacy consent tracking
* Secure data handling

### **WORKFLOW SUMMARY**

#### **Complete App Creation Process**

**Step 1: App Info**

1. Enter app name (max 30 characters)
2. Upload app logo (PNG, 1MB max, 1024x1024)
3. Click Save

**Step 2: App Design**

**Splash Screen:**

1. Upload splash screen image
2. Select background color
3. Click Save

**Theme:**

1. Select theme color
2. Click Save

**Onboarding:**

1. Create 2-5 onboarding screens
2. Add label text for each
3. Upload image for each (PNG, 1MB max, 768x1024)
4. Use "Add New Screen" for additional screens
5. Click Save

**Login Screen:**

1. Upload login screen logo
2. Enter Terms and Conditions URL
3. Enter Privacy Policy URL
4. Click Save

**Step 3: Prepare Submission**

**Test Account:**

1. Enter test student email
2. Click Save

**App Store:**

1. Check "Publish to App Store"
2. Enter subtitle
3. Enter description
4. Enter support URL
5. Enter copyright
6. Click Save

**Play Store:**

1. Check "Publish to Play Store"
2. Enter email address
3. Enter phone number (optional)
4. Enter website
5. Enter short description
6. Enter full description
7. Click Save

**Step 4: Build App**

1. Confirm Apple Developer Business Account registration
2. Enter Entity Name and Team ID
3. Confirm Google Play Console Business Account registration
4. Enter Organisation name and Account ID
5. Confirm Firebase account creation
6. Enter Project name and Project ID
7. Click "Build and Publish the App"

### **VISUAL DESIGN ELEMENTS**

#### **Color Scheme**

* **Primary Purple:** Used for buttons, active states
* **Light Purple/Grey:** Table headers, inactive backgrounds
* **Yellow:** App theme color in Branding section (`#ffd12a`)

* **Dark Grey/Black:** Text
* **Medium Grey:** Helper text, character counters

* **Secondary Action:** White background, border ("Reset")

* **Text Area:** Multi-line, white background
* **Dropdown:** White background with dropdown arrow
* **Character Counters:** Grey text, positioned top right of fields

### **FUNCTIONAL WORKFLOW**

#### **Branding Configuration**

1. Navigate to Site → Mobile App → Branding
2. Upload app logo (150x150)
3. Select app theme color using color picker or enter hex code
4. View changes in App Preview panel
5. Click "Save" to apply changes
6. Click "Reset" to revert changes

#### **Send Push Notification**

1. Navigate to Site → Mobile App → Push Notifications
2. Enter notification title (max 100 characters)
3. Enter message content (max 2400 characters)
4. Select recipients:

   * "Send to all members" OR
   * "Send to specific members"
5. Select action when clicked:

   * No action (Select)
   * Go to a course
   * Go to a funnel
   * Go to a URL
6. Click "Send"
7. Notification appears in "Sent Messages" table

#### **View Sent Notification**

1. Locate notification in "Sent Messages" table
2. Click eye icon in Actions column
3. View notification details (Title, Message, Recipients, Action)
4. See linked URL if action was configured

### **Program Description**

#### **Main Text**

**Content:** "With our Partner Program we offer the best referral commissions in the industry. Refer a friend and make **40%** of what they pay Zenler - we call this **Tier 1** commissions. If that friend refers another friend you will get **5%** of what they pay Zenler as well - we call this **Tier 2** Commissions. And this is not just for first year, it's forever as long as referrals stay with Zenler. Get your referral link/URL in Partner Settings to get started."

**Formatting:**

* Bold text for percentages (40%, 5%)
* Bold text for tier names (Tier 1, Tier 2)
* Centered paragraph

### **VISUAL DESIGN ELEMENTS**

#### **Color Scheme**

* **Purple:** Primary actions, charts, icons, toggle switches
* **Light Purple:** Card backgrounds, table headers
* **Blue:** Links, informational text
* **White:** Content areas, buttons
* **Grey:** Text, borders

#### **Typography**

* **Headings:** Bold, large
* **Body Text:** Regular weight
* **Values:** Bold, prominent
* **Helper Text:** Smaller, grey

#### **Icons**

* Dollar signs for financial metrics
* Person icons for user counts
* Information icons for tooltips
* Copy icons for copyable fields

### **FUNCTIONAL WORKFLOW**

#### **Join Partner Program**

1. Click "PARTNER PROGRAM" in sidebar
2. View program overview and commission structure (40% Tier 1, 5% Tier 2)
3. Click "Access the Partner Dashboard"
4. Review Terms \& Conditions

#### **View Earnings**

1. Navigate to Partner Dashboard
2. View earning cards (Today, Last 7 Days, This Month, Last 30 Days)
3. Review Overview section metrics
4. Check Earnings chart for trends

#### **Access Referral Link**

1. Navigate to Partner Settings
2. View Partner URL field
3. Click copy icon to copy referral link
4. Share link with network

#### **Invite Friends**

1. Click "Invite a Friend" button in sidebar
2. Modal opens with referral URL
3. Copy URL using copy button
4. Share via email, social media, or direct message
5. Click Cancel to close modal

#### **Setup Payment**

1. Navigate to Payment Settings
2. Enter PayPal email address
3. Click Save
4. Notification banner dismisses after setup

#### **Review Transactions**

1. View Transactions table in Partner Dashboard
2. Use filter dropdown to select:

   * All transactions
   * Tier1 only
   * Tier2 only
3. Use date filter for specific periods
4. Search for specific referrals using search box
5. Export data using Export(CSV) button

#### **Review Signups**

1. View Site Signups table
2. Apply filters (All, Tier1, Tier2)
3. Use date filter as needed
4. Search for specific signups
5. Track Tier1 vs Tier2 signups in summary
6. Export data for analysis

#### **Monitor Commission Status**

1. Check Transactions table
2. Review Pay-Out Status column
3. Identify "Not Paid" entries
4. Track when status changes to "Paid"
5. Verify earnings in Paid metric (Overview section)

#### **Toggle Partner Badge**

1. Navigate to Partner Settings
2. Locate Partner Badge field
3. Click toggle switch to turn On/Off
4. When On: Badge displays on partner's site
5. When Off: Badge hidden from site

\---

### **COMMISSION STRUCTURE**

#### **Tier 1 Commissions**

**Rate:** 40% **Applied To:** Direct referrals (people you refer) **Calculation:** 40% of what referred user pays Zenler **Duration:** Forever (as long as referral stays with Zenler)

#### **Tier 2 Commissions**

**Rate:** 5% **Applied To:** Indirect referrals (people your referrals refer) **Calculation:** 5% of what second-level user pays Zenler **Duration:** Forever (as long as referral stays with Zenler)

#### **Example Scenario**

* You refer User A (Tier 1)
* User A pays Zenler $100/month
* You earn: $40/month (40% of $100)
* User A refers User B (Tier 2 for you)
* User B pays Zenler $100/month
* You earn: $5/month (5% of $100)
* **Total Monthly Earnings:** $45
